### [1] [Human-Centric Intelligence in the Era of Foundation Models: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18184)
*Yang Chen,Tianqi Wang,Xiaorui Jiang,Yilei Man,Yihua Shao,Mengyuan Liu,Zhi Chen,Xiaofeng Cao,Qibin Zhao,Chi Harold Liu,Albert Y. Zomaya,Nicu Sebe,Jingren Zhou,Dacheng Tao,Song Guo,Jingcai Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"全谱人类上下文分类法"（full-spectrum human context taxonomy），将人类中心智能整合为六个相互关联的层级——从可观测主体（视觉外观、空间几何）、动态执行者（运动学动力学、交互建模）到情境化智能体（世界模拟、具身智能）——并系统综述了该领域的方法基础、代表性方法、数据集与基准，最后讨论了开放挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基础模型时代，人类中心智能虽然强调规模、可迁移性和通用建模，但尚未像基础模型那样取得同等进展。更重要的是，该领域各方向的进展分散在不同任务、模态和研究社区中，其内在的概念与方法论联系尚不清晰，亟需一个统一框架来弥合这些分歧。

Method: 作者提出一个六层级全谱人类上下文分类法，将人类分别视为：(1)可观测主体（视觉外观与空间几何）、(2)动态执行者（运动学动力学与交互建模）、(3)情境化智能体（世界模拟与具身智能）。随后阐述该领域的方法论基础，包括人类中心数据族、计算架构范式和训练/推理优化策略，并系统综述各层级的代表性方法及对应的数据集、基准与评估指标。

Result: 系统性组织了人类中心智能领域的文献，建立了跨任务、跨模态、跨社区的统一分类框架，整理了相关的数据集、基准和评估指标，并维护了一个持续更新的项目页面作为文献与资源集合。

Conclusion: 作者旨在为人类中心智能提供一个连贯的框架和实用参考，指出实现可扩展、可信赖、物理扎根且可部署的人类中心智能所面临的开放挑战，并规划了有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Human-centric intelligence is evolving in the foundation-model era, with growing emphasis on scale, transferability, and general-purpose modeling. Yet it has not fully integrated with foundation models to achieve the comparable progress seen in them. More importantly, recent advances across this broad landscape remain fragmented across tasks, modalities, and research communities, leaving their intrinsic conceptual and methodological connections unclear. To bridge these divides and rethink human-centric intelligence in the foundation-model era, we introduce a full-spectrum human context taxonomy that integrates six interconnected levels by viewing humans as observable subjects through visual appearance and spatial geometry, as dynamic actors through kinematic dynamics and interaction modeling, and as situated agents through world simulation and embodied agency. We next present the methodological foundations of the field, covering human-centric data families, computational architecture paradigms, and representative training and inference optimization strategies. We then systematically review representative methods across these levels and organize the associated datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation metrics. We further discuss open challenges and promising research directions toward human-centric intelligence that is scalable, trustworthy, physically grounded, and deployable, aiming to provide a coherent framework and practical reference for advancing the field. Finally, we provide a systematically organized and continuously updated collection of human-centric AI literature and resources on our project page.

</details>


### [2] [Bound-Aware Per-Organ Recall Risk Control for Multi-Organ CT Segmentation under Clinical Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18193)
*Souraj Adhikary,Negar Chabi,Andre Mastmeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨无分布假设的风险控制方法，为预训练分割模型（AMOS训练的nnU-Net）添加各器官特异性的召回率保证。通过校准每器官阈值，作者验证了在源数据集上的控制效果，但发现模型迁移到RAOS数据集后，12个器官中有7个超过了α=0.10的误差阈值。文章对比了RCPS、CRC和WSR等不同风险控制方法，评估了重新认证所需的本地病例数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练分割模型在部署前需要保证其风险（如召回率）有统计学保证，但现有方法多为固定阈值或仅提供整体保证，缺乏针对各器官的个性化风险控制。此外，模型从源域迁移到目标域时，性能往往退化，需要评估重新认证的成本与可行性。

Method: 作者为AMOS训练的nnU-Net校准每器官阈值以控制均体素假阴性率（FNR）风险；对比了RCPS（高概率控制总体均值风险）和CRC（期望控制）两种方法，并引入WSR投注界作为新的重认证工具，与传统的Hoeffding-Bentkus界进行比较。通过本地少量病例评估各器官的重认证成本。

Result: AMOS上的控制通过，但迁移到RAOS后7/12器官超过α=0.10阈值，且小校准集可能掩盖违规。WSR界能以25个本地病例重新认证6个Tier-1器官，优于HB的30-40例；CRC仅需10-15例但个体尾部风险较重。Tier-2器官在25例下无法满足精度标准。

Conclusion: 分布外迁移会破坏原始风险控制保证；WSR投注界在小样本重认证场景中比HB更高效，但需要根据器官层级和精度要求选择合适的控制方法。不存在统一最优方案，需在保证强度（高概率vs期望）与样本效率之间权衡。

Abstract: Distribution-free risk control adds organ-specific recall guarantees to frozen segmentation. We calibrate per-organ thresholds for an AMOS-trained nnU-Net, audit transfer to RAOS, and estimate local re-certification cost using case-level voxel false-negative rate (FNR). The AMOS control passes, but $7/12$ organs exceed $α{=}0.10$ after transfer; smaller calibration sets can mask exceedances with conservative or vacuous thresholds. Risk-Controlling Prediction Sets (RCPS) give high-probability control of population-mean risk, whereas Conformal Risk Control (CRC) gives weaker expectation control. Both require exchangeability; fixed and global thresholds give no per-organ guarantee. The Waudby--Smith--Ramdas (WSR) betting bound re-certifies six Tier-1 organs with 25 local cases, versus 30--40 for Hoeffding--Bentkus (HB). CRC needs 10--15 but has a heavier individual-case tail. No Tier-2 organ meets our illustrative precision criterion with 25 cases.

</details>


### [3] [LumiTokens: 3D Relighting via Token-Space Lighting Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18215)
*Yiwen Chen,Matheus Gadelha,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LumiTokens提出了一种全新的3D重照明框架，直接在潜在场景token上进行变换，无需显式3D表示、渲染方程或物理分解。通过Scene Token Editor联合处理场景token与光射线token（Plücker ray tokens），支持多种光源类型，并能实现渐进式、可组合的照明编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重照明方法（显式材质分解、扩散视图生成或两者结合）每次改变光照条件都需要完全重新计算，效率低下且缺乏灵活性。作者观察到近期潜在场景表示（将多视角图像编码为紧凑token，无固定物理语义）为重照明开辟了新设计空间。

Method: 将3D重照明建模为对潜在场景token的直接变换。核心是Scene Token Editor，通过自注意力机制联合处理场景token与光射线token，输出更新后的token可解码为多视角一致的重照明图像。所有光源信号（环境图、点光源、面光源）统一参数化为Plücker ray tokens，支持原生3D用户交互且无显式空间结构。

Result: 实验表明LumiTokens在重照明质量上达到或超越现有方法，并支持渐进式、可组合的照明编辑——用户可逐个添加光源，每次编辑在token空间中自然叠加。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了潜在场景表示作为重照明媒介的可行性，通过端到端token变换避免了物理分解的复杂性，同时实现了轻量、灵活的交互式照明编辑能力。

Abstract: Existing 3D relighting methods operate through either explicit material decomposition, diffusion-based view-space generation, or a combination of both, requiring full recomputation for each new lighting condition. We observe that recent latent scene representations, which encode multi-view images into a set of compact tokens with no fixed physical semantics, open up a novel design space for relighting. We present LumiTokens, a framework that formulates 3D relighting as a direct transformation on latent scene tokens, without explicit 3D representations, rendering equations, or physics-based decomposition. Our model introduces a Scene Token Editor that processes scene tokens jointly with light-ray tokens through self-attention, producing updated tokens that can be decoded into multi-view-consistent relit images. To support diverse lighting types through a unified interface, all lighting signals, including environment maps, point lights, and area lights, are parameterized as Plucker ray tokens, enabling native 3D user interaction with a representation that carries no explicit spatial structure. Crucially, this design supports progressive relighting: because the editor's output remains in the same latent space as its input, a user can incrementally build up illumination one light source at a time, with each edit composing in token space. Experiments demonstrate that LumiTokens achieves comparable or superior relighting quality to other methods and supports progressive, composable lighting edits. Project page: https://neu-vi.github.io/LumiTokens/

</details>


### [4] [Zero-Shot Transfer of Force Map Estimation Across GelSight Mini Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18240)
*Julio Castaño Amoros,Pablo Gil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨GelSight Mini传感器单元（跨版本）泛化估计3D力映射图的方法，通过两阶段实现：先使用UniT模型将输入触觉图像重建为通用触觉图像（域适应），再使用U-Net网络估计3D力映射图，在图像重建阶段SSIM达0.9338±0.0358，力估计阶段MAE_F为1.1294±1.5934(N)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感器虽已工业化制造，但大多数传感器仍在研究实验室手工制作，这导致传感器性能难以标准化，每个生产单元都需要重复收集数据和训练模型。为了解决跨不同GelSight Mini传感器单元（不论版本）的3D力映射图估计泛化问题。

Method: 方法包含两个阶段：（1）域适应阶段，利用UniT模型将输入的触觉图像重建为通用触觉图像，消除不同传感器单元间的差异；（2）力估计阶段，使用U-Net网络从重建后的通用触觉图像估计3D力映射图。

Result: 图像重建阶段SSIM达到0.9338±0.0358，力估计阶段MAE_F为1.1294±1.5934(N)，两个阶段均取得有前景的结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段方法能够实现跨不同GelSight Mini传感器单元的3D力映射图估计泛化，解决了传感器差异导致的重复数据收集和模型训练问题。

Abstract: Despite the rapid industrialization of the touch sensor manufacturing process, most of these sensors are still handmade in research laboratories. This complicates standardizing their performance, requiring the repetition of data collection and training models for each unit produced. To address this problem, this paper presents a method that can generalize the estimation of 3D force maps across different GelSight Mini sensor units, regardless of the sensor version. Specifically, the method consists of two stages: a domain adaptation stage, in which the input tactile image is reconstructed as a general tactile image using a UniT-based model; and a stage for estimating 3D force maps employing a U-Net network. Our proposal achieves promising results in both steps, such as an SSIM of 0.9338 +- 0.0358 in the image reconstruction phase and an MAE_F of 1.1294 +- 1.5934(N) in the force estimation phase.

</details>


### [5] [Visual-Prompt Guided Wildlife Instance-Level Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18246)
*Mufhumudzi Muthivhi,Jiahao Huo,Terence van Zyl,Fredrik Gustafsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种单阶段端到端的野生动物重识别模型，将目标检测与身份识别统一在潜在空间中进行。模型采用DINOv2提取稳健的空间几何特征，并结合MegaDescriptor进行野生动物重识别，通过提示重识别特征增强潜在查询，由检测解码器在场景潜在空间中建立目标边界。初步实验取得30.584%的mAP，而两阶段SOTA方法为44.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度野生动物重识别具有挑战性，现有SOTA方法采用"检测+重识别"的两阶段流水线，这种方法步骤分离、效率较低且可能存在误差累积问题，因此需要探索更高效的单阶段端到端方案。

Method: 提出一种单阶段端到端的检测与重识别联合模型：1) 采用DINOv2提取潜在空间中的稳健空间几何特征；2) 使用MegaDescriptor进行野生动物重识别；3) 通过提示重识别特征增强潜在查询；4) 检测解码器在场景潜在空间内为特定目标身份建立物体边界框。

Result: 初步结果显示该方法获得30.584%的mAP，与两阶段SOTA方法的44.89%相比具有竞争力。定性结果表明模型能够有效地对动物身份进行边界框定和识别。

Conclusion: 单阶段端到端方法在野生动物重识别领域展现出可行性和潜力，虽在mAP上与两阶段方法存在差距，但验证了将检测与重识别统一在潜在空间中的有效性，为后续性能提升提供了研究方向。

Abstract: Fine-grained wildlife re-identification remains a challenging area in research. Current state-of-the-art approaches apply a detection and re-identification pipeline. We propose a one-stage end-to-end detection and re-identification model that performs identity searching within the latent space. We adopt DINOv2 for robust spatial geometry and MegaDescriptor for wildlife re-identification. We enhance latent queries with prompt re-identification features. A detection decoder queries the scene latent space to establish object boundaries around the target identity. Preliminary findings reflect a competitive mean average precision score of 30.584% compared to the state-of-the-art two stage approach of 44.89%. Qualitative results depict effective bounding and identification of animal identities.

</details>


### [6] [Acquisition Geometry-Assisted Whole-Group Localization of X-ray Fluorescence Maps in Optical Microscopy Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18305)
*Xiangyu Yin,Tatjana Paunesku,Letonia Copeland-Hardin,Martina Ralle,Zichao Wendy Di,Si Chen,Gayle E. Woloschak,Barry Lai,Mathew J. Cherukara,Stefan Vogt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文正式化了XRF（X射线荧光）瓷砖组定位问题，即利用采集几何约束为一组XRF tiles估计单一光学图像位置，以GroupIoU量化。对照实验表明独立定位完全失败（GroupIoU=0.000），而组定位达到0.931；使用互信息替代归一化互相关结果几乎相同；多尺度案例中利用粗扫描连接细尺度tile组，使平均GroupIoU从0.694提升至0.856。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XRF显微镜与光学显微镜模态在对比机制和分辨率上存在差异，导致XRF视场在光学图像中的定位困难。现有工作流大多独立放置每个XRF tile，即使采集元数据已记录tiles间的相对扫描位置，这一约束被浪费，缺乏对XRF tile组定位问题的正式化定义与量化评估。

Method: 提出XRF tile组定位的正式框架：为整个tile组估计单一光学框架位置，以采集几何作为显式约束，并用GroupIoU量化定位质量。在受控案例中比较独立定位与组定位；通过将归一化互相关替换为互信息验证结果对相似性度量的鲁棒性；在多尺度案例中引入粗XRF调查扫描作为桥梁连接细尺度tile组与光学图像。

Result: 受控案例中独立定位失败（GroupIoU=0.000），组定位达到0.931；MI与NCC结果几乎相同；多尺度案例中利用粗扫描使平均GroupIoU从0.694提升至0.856。

Conclusion: 案例研究支持在定位相关XRF tiles时将采集几何作为显式约束，组定位方法显著优于独立定位，且结果对局部相似性度量选择不敏感。

Abstract: X-ray fluorescence (XRF) microscopy maps elemental distributions, while optical microscopy can provide complementary morphological context. Localizing XRF fields of view (FOVs) in optical images is difficult because the two modalities differ in contrast mechanism and resolution. Most current workflows place each XRF tile independently, even when acquisition metadata already record the tiles' relative scan positions. This study formalizes XRF tile-group localization, in which one optical-frame placement is estimated for the whole group, constrained by acquisition geometry and quantified using group intersection-over-union (GroupIoU). In a controlled case study, independent localization failed with GroupIoU 0.000, whereas group localization achieved 0.931. Replacing the normalized cross-correlation (NCC) metric with mutual information (MI) gave nearly identical results, showing that the outcome is not specific to one local similarity metric. In another multiscale case study, using a coarse XRF survey scan to connect the fine-scale tile group to the optical image increased mean GroupIoU from 0.694 to 0.856. These case studies support using acquisition geometry as an explicit constraint when localizing related XRF tiles.

</details>


### [7] [High-Flux Count-Free Single-Photon 3D Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18306)
*Kaustubh Sadekar,Vivek K Goyal,David Maier,Atul Ingle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合自由运行采集与分析合成软件流水线的计算方法，用于在带宽受限的单光子雪崩二极管（SPAD）相机中缓解堆积（pile-up）失真，使其能够在高通量（high-flux）现实场景下可靠工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPAD单光子相机在3D传感中应用广泛，但面临两大挑战：(i) 高通量条件下产生非线性"堆积"失真；(ii) 原始光子数据量巨大造成每像素的数据瓶颈。虽然压缩感知技术可缓解数据传输问题，但会因无法保留足够的光子探测历史信息而使现有堆积校正方法失效，因此需要新方法兼顾数据压缩与失真校正。

Method: 提出一种结合自由运行捕获（free-running capture）与分析-合成（analysis-by-synthesis）软件流水线的计算成像方法，通过该流水线对光子探测过程进行建模与仿真，从而在后处理阶段缓解堆积失真。

Result: 通过硬件仿真和全场景/单像素模拟实验证明，该方法能够在广泛的照明条件下可靠地捕获场景距离和反射率信息。

Conclusion: 该工作将使严重带宽受限的高分辨率SPAD相机能够在现实高光子通量场景中运行，推动SPAD相机在真实世界中的广泛应用。

Abstract: Single-photon cameras based on single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) technology are gaining popularity for 3D sensing, thanks to their extreme sensitivity and time resolution. There are two key challenges with single-photon cameras that limit their widespread use: (i) they suffer from non-linear distortions called ''pile-up'' when operated in high-photon-flux conditions, and (ii) they generate a large volume of raw photon data, creating a severe data bottleneck at each sensor pixel. In this work, we show that while compressive capture techniques successfully mitigate data transfer challenges, they exacerbate the effects of dead-time distortion because they fail to retain sufficient information about the photon detection history to allow post-processing pile-up correction via existing methods. We propose a new computational-imaging method that combines free-running capture with an analysis-by-synthesis software pipeline to mitigate pile-up distortions. Our results with hardware emulations and full-scene and single-pixel simulations show that our method can reliably capture scene distance and reflectance over a wide range of illumination conditions. Our work will enable high-resolution SPAD cameras that are severely bandwidth-constrained to operate in real-world high-flux scenarios.

</details>


### [8] [XRF-to-Optical Field-of-View Localization with Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18309)
*Xiangyu Yin,Tatjana Paunesku,Letonia Copeland-Hardin,Martina Ralle,Zichao Wendy Di,Si Chen,Gayle E. Woloschak,Barry Lai,Mathew J. Cherukara,Stefan Vogt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了无需训练的视觉语言模型（VLM）在关联X射线荧光（XRF）与光学显微镜图像中的视场（FOV）定位能力，提出并验证了一种"提案-验证"工作流，在跨模态低对应场景下恢复了有效的定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在相关XRF与光学显微镜成像中，XRF图通常只覆盖光学图像的小区域，需要FOV定位。但不同模态间外观和结构差异大，传统方法难以可靠定位，需要探索新的训练-free定位方法。

Method: 在两个数据集（同切片高对应与相邻切片低对应）上，测试无约束和元数据约束的VLM定位；与几何对照、经典模板匹配、DINOv2和多GradICON等训练-free方法比较；并提出提案-验证工作流，用重复VLM预测作为候选，基于图像相似性选择最终位置。

Result: 直接VLM提示产生内容依赖的空间信号但单独不可靠；经典匹配在跨模态结构保留时最准确，但在低对应数据集失败；提案-验证工作流在低对应场景恢复了有用的定位能力。

Conclusion: 训练-free VLM单独定位不可靠，但结合提案-验证策略能够在跨模态低对应的困难场景下实现有效FOV定位，为无需训练的跨模态图像配准提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Registering images acquired with different microscopy modalities is essential for relating complementary measurements of the same specimen. In correlative X-ray fluorescence (XRF) and optical microscopy, the XRF map often covers only a small region of an optical image acquired from the same or an adjacent tissue section. Field-of-view (FOV) localization is necessary but can be difficult when appearance and structure differ across modalities. Here we evaluate training-free vision language model (VLM) localization on two datasets representing same-section high-correspondence and adjacent-section low-correspondence imaging. We test unconstrained and metadata-constrained search and compare VLMs with geometric controls, classical template matching, and two alternative training-free approaches (DINOv2 and multiGradICON). Direct VLM prompting produced content-dependent spatial signals but was not reliable alone. Classical matching was most accurate when cross-modal structure was preserved but failed in the low-correspondence collection. A proposal-and-verify workflow used repeated VLM predictions as candidates and image-based similarity to select the final location. This workflow recovered useful localization in the low-correspondence regime.

</details>


### [9] [FedCoRe: Target-Adaptive Completion for Missing Modalities in Healthcare Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18311)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Peter Cnudde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCoRe（联邦跨模态表示补全）框架，用于解决联邦多模态学习中各站点模态不完全一致的问题。该方法通过学习表示空间或logit空间的修正而非生成合成数据，通过配对样本监督训练补全模块，在MIMIC呼吸恶化任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中不同医院拥有的模态数据不同（如EHR、胸片、ECG），而现有联邦多模态模型假设所有站点都有全部模态，这一假设不切实际。作者希望解决模态缺失情况下联邦多模态预测性能下降的问题。

Method: FedCoRe框架：1）在表示空间或logit空间学习修正而非生成合成ECG/CXR图像；2）当客户端观测到部署时可能缺失的模态时，对相同样本在有/无该模态两种条件下评估，获得配对监督；3）仅拥有配对样本的客户端更新补全模块；4）验证阶段可保留未改变的预测；5）冻结已训练的多模态预测器，确保测量差异仅来自补全模块。

Result: 隐藏ECG导致AUROC下降约0.085；配对示例FedAvg恢复0.0415 AUROC（恢复49.0%的性能损失）。两个不同效应被报告：配对示例FedAvg部分恢复缺失ECG的差距，而验证选择的补全是任务特异的分类器logit修正而非真正的ECG恢复。对于CXR，效应感知补全在受控测试中恢复52.8%的损失。

Conclusion: FedCoRe应被视为验证门控的补全/修正框架：它能在受支持的设置中恢复缺失模态信号，但仅在配对示例和验证证据支持该模态时才应部署。该框架不追求模态的完整重建，而是通过修正机制在有限条件下提升预测性能。

Abstract: Federated multimodal models often assume every site has every modality, although hospitals differ in access to EHRs, chest radiographs, and ECGs. We study this setting on a MIMIC-derived respiratory deterioration task with simulated FL clients and introduce FedCoRe (Federated Cross-Modal Representation Completion). FedCoRe learns representation- or logit-space corrections rather than generating synthetic ECGs or CXR images. When a client observes a modality that may be missing at deployment, it evaluates the same example with and without that modality to obtain paired supervision. Only clients with such pairs update the completion module, and validation may retain the unchanged prediction. We freeze the trained multimodal predictor during evaluation so that measured differences come only from completion. Hiding ECG reduced AUROC by about 0.085; paired-example FedAvg restored 0.0415 AUROC, or 49.0% of the lost performance. We therefore report two distinct effects: paired-example FedAvg partially recovers the missing-ECG gap, while validation-selected completion is a task-specific classifier-logit correction rather than literal ECG recovery. For CXR, effect-aware completion recovers 52.8% of the loss in a controlled test where CXR is hidden. Paired-example FedAvg transfers part of this effect, but validation keeps the no-completion baseline for deployment cases whose inputs lack CXR. Thus, FedCoRe should be read as a validation-gated completion/correction framework: it can recover missing-modality signal in supported settings, but it should be deployed only when paired examples and validation evidence support that modality.

</details>


### [10] [A Configurable Privacy-Preserving MRI Processing Workflow Using Deep Learning-Based Brain Extraction and Adaptive Anatomical Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18316)
*Rayeef Ali Khan,Komal Raj Mahantesh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可配置的隐私保护MRI处理工作流，通过扩展基于深度学习的脑提取方法（SynthStrip），实现自适应解剖保留、交互式保留选择和集成质量控制，在保护面部/颅骨隐私信息的同时提供灵活的解剖保真度平衡，并在IXI数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构MRI数据中可能保留面部和颅骨的解剖信息，存在隐私泄露风险。现有基于深度学习的脑提取方法通常只产生单一固定输出，无法在不同隐私保护与解剖保留需求之间灵活权衡，限制了其在不同应用场景中的适用性。

Method: 该方法构建了一个可配置的隐私保护MRI处理工作流：(1)使用SynthStrip进行自动化脑提取；(2)通过形态学掩膜扩展生成可配置的基于壳层（shell-based）的保留级别；(3)开发交互式保留框架（Interactive Preservation Framework）让用户比较和选择适当的保留配置；(4)集成质量控制框架提供多平面可视化和脑掩膜叠加验证。实现使用Python和开源神经影像库，部署在Renku可复现研究环境中。

Result: 在公开的IXI数据集上使用结构T1加权MRI数据进行了评估，实验结果表明能够实现解剖学上合理的脑提取和可配置的保留输出，并通过系统的可视化验证得到了支持。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是一个模块化、可复现的MRI预处理框架，将深度学习的脑提取能力与可配置的解剖保留、交互式用户引导处理和集成质量控制相结合，为隐私导向的神经影像研究和协作式医学图像分析提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Structural Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is widely used in neuroimaging research and clinical practice, but structural MRI volumes may retain facial and cranial anatomical information that raises privacy concerns. Existing deep learning-based brain extraction methods generally produce a single fixed output, limiting flexibility when different applications require different balances between privacy and anatomical preservation. This paper presents a configurable privacy-preserving MRI processing workflow that extends deep learning-based brain extraction through adaptive anatomical preservation, interactive preservation selection, and integrated quality control. The workflow employs SynthStrip for automated brain extraction, followed by morphological mask expansion to generate configurable shell-based preservation levels. An Interactive Preservation Framework enables users to compare preservation configurations and select an appropriate output, while an integrated Quality Control Framework provides multi-plane visualisation and brain-mask overlay verification. The workflow was implemented in Python using open-source neuroimaging libraries within the Renku reproducible research environment and evaluated using structural T1-weighted MRI data from the publicly available IXI dataset. Experimental results demonstrate anatomically plausible brain extraction and configurable preservation outputs, supported by systematic visual verification. The principal contribution is a modular and reproducible MRI preprocessing framework that enhances deep learning-based brain extraction with configurable anatomical preservation, interactive user-guided processing, and integrated quality control. The workflow provides a practical foundation for privacy-oriented neuroimaging research and collaborative medical image analysis.

</details>


### [11] [Reproducible Multimodal Affordance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18317)
*Tommaso Apicella,Alessio Xompero,Andrea Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Affordance Sheet"的文档化工具，用于记录多功能预测（affordance prediction）任务的详细规范，包括任务定义、输入模态、模型架构、训练信息、数据集和实验协议，以解决该领域评估和比较困难的问题，促进可复现的基准测试和可靠评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多功能预测方法的评估与比较存在诸多困难：异构的问题表述、不一致的数据集标注、实验协议报告不完整，以及部署条件信息有限。这些局限性阻碍了公平的基准测试和性能比较，需要一种标准化的文档机制来提升透明度。

Method: 提出Affordance Sheet，一种文档化规范，详细记录以下内容：任务表述及输入模态、模型架构和训练信息、数据集信息、实验协议。通过标准化的文档模板来规范多功能预测研究的记录和报告。

Result: Affordance Sheet能够实现可复现的基准测试和对多功能模型在真实世界场景下的可靠评估，包括对新颖条件的泛化能力和人类安全性方面的考量。

Conclusion: 该研究强调通过系统性文档化来改善多功能预测领域的可复现性和评估公平性，为真实世界部署（含泛化性和安全性）提供标准化评估框架。

Abstract: Affordance prediction is the identification of potential actions an agent can perform on a target object from multimodal inputs. Affordance prediction methods are difficult to evaluate and compare due to heterogeneous problem formulations, inconsistent dataset annotations, incomplete reporting of experimental protocols, and limited information about deployment conditions. These limitations challenge fair benchmarking and performance comparison. To promote transparency, we propose the Affordance Sheet, a documentation detailing task formulation with its input modalities, model architectures and training information, datasets, and experimental protocols. Affordance Sheets enable reproducible benchmarking and reliable evaluation of affordance models for real-world scenarios, including generalisation to novel conditions and human safety.

</details>


### [12] [From Inference to Adaptation: A Unified Optimal Transport View of Vision Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18339)
*Qi Yu,Zhichen Zeng,Katherine Tieu,Xiyuan Yang,Ruizhong Qiu,Yuchen Yan,Lihui Liu,Yanjun Zhao,Lingjie Chen,Jingrui He,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Wasserstein最优传输的VLM测试时自适应（TTA）方法，通过将零样本推理和测试时自适应统一到同一目标框架下，利用OT生成的鲁棒伪标签结合软标签InfoNCE损失进行细粒度跨模态对齐，解决了传统TTA方法中伪标签不可靠和目标错配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的VLM测试时自适应方法依赖原始嵌入相似度直接预测的伪标签，在分布偏移下不可靠并会放大噪声；而现有方法为了避免噪声放大采用粗粒度的代理目标，无法显式建模不同模态间的样本级关系，导致推理与自适应的目标不匹配，性能提升有限。

Method: 1) 将零样本图像分类建模为通过Wasserstein最优传输编码的跨模态对齐问题，在样本级提供鲁棒伪标签；2) 采用软标签InfoNCE损失基于OT诱导的伪标签进行VLM自适应，通过对比学习显式建模个体图像-文本对的关系；3) 理论上证明InfoNCE损失可以重新表述为Wasserstein OT形式，从而统一推理和自适应的目标。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在效果和效率上均优于现有方法，相比最佳方法性能提升最高达7%，同时具备最先进的效率。

Conclusion: 通过理论上统一VLM推理和自适应目标，实现了二者的相互促进，为VLM在分布偏移下的测试时自适应提供了原理性的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable zero-shot capabilities yet remain sensitive to real-world distribution shifts during inference. Although significant efforts are devoted to adapting VLMs at test time, they rely heavily on noisy pseudo-labels predicted directly from raw embedding similarities during inference, which are unreliable under distribution shift and mislead the adaptation. To avoid noise amplification, existing works craft coarse-grained surrogate objectives during adaptation, which fail to explicitly model sample-level relationships across different modalities, creating objective mismatch with inference, thus leading to marginal performance improvement. In this work, we aim to bridge the detached objectives of inference and adaptation for VLMs, and propose a principled VLM TTA method called \algname. For VLM inference, we formulate the zero-shot image classification task as a cross-modal alignment problem encoded via a Wasserstein OT formulation, providing robust pseudo-labels at the sample-level to effectively adapt VLMs. For VLM adaptation, we adopt a soft-label InfoNCE loss to adapt VLMs based on the OT-induced pseudo-labels, leveraging fine-grained supervisions to explicitly model relationships of individual image-text pairs via contrastive learning, which empowers accurate inference at the same granularity. Moreover, we theoretically reveal that the InfoNCE loss can be neatly reformulated as a Wasserstein OT formulation, thereby unifying the objectives of the inference and adaptation of VLMs to achieve their mutual benefits. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our methods, outperforming the best-performing methods by up to 7% with state-of-the-art efficiency.

</details>


### [13] [TTSD-FAR: Test-Time Self-Distillation with Fisher-Anchored Restoration for Missing-Modality Emotion Recognition in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18386)
*Muhammad Haseeb Aslam,Alessandro Koerich,Marco Pedersoli,Ali Etemad,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TTSD（Test-Time Self-Distillation）框架，结合Fisher-Anchored Restoration (FAR)机制，解决大型视频语言模型（LVLMs）在多模态情感识别中面临的部分模态缺失/噪声问题，实现持续稳定的测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别本质上是多模态的，但真实部署中模态可能缺失或含噪。现有TTA方法（如熵最小化、困惑度降低）不适用于自回归LVLM，RAG在弱模态下会退化。由于缺乏真值监督，持续自适应容易累积漂移。需要同时应对任意缺失模态模式并保持持续自适应的有效性。

Method: TTSD采用参数高效框架：冻结的教师模型（在完整模态上训练）通过自蒸馏引导自适应的低秩学生模型，仅更新极少量参数。FAR机制监控Fisher信息稳定性，检测收敛与漂移，当识别到分布偏移时将学生模型恢复到教师的锚点状态。该统一的自适应-恢复设计同时解决模态缺失和持续漂移问题。

Result: 在MELD、DFEW和BAH数据集上，在0%-50%模态缺失条件下，TTSD-FAR在长时间自适应场景中一致优于基于熵的自适应方法、RAG和基于困惑度的生成方法。基线方法在无恢复机制时逐步退化，而TTSD-FAR保持稳定一致的表现。

Conclusion: 统一的自适应-恢复设计（TTSD-FAR）能够有效解决LVLMs在部分模态缺失情况下的持续测试时自适应问题，通过教师蒸馏和Fisher信息监控实现了稳定且参数高效的适应，优于现有TTA和RAG方法。

Abstract: Large video-language models (LVLMs) have shown remarkable performance on multimodal tasks like multimodal emotion recognition (ER) in the wild. ER is inherently multimodal, requiring a joint understanding of facial expressions, vocalizations, language, biosignals, and gestures. However, real-world deployment remains challenging: modalities may be missing or noisy at test time. Partial observations can be viewed as a distribution shift relative to the complete-modality distribution. SOTA TTA methods based on entropy minimization or perplexity reduction do not transfer to autoregressive LVLMs, while retrieval augmented generation (RAG) degrades when the observed modality is weak. Because no ground-truth supervision exists to verify individual updates, adaptation across this stream risks accumulating drift and degrading once the model departs from a reliable solution. An effective solution must therefore adapt to arbitrary missing-modality patterns and remain effective during continual adaptation. We address both jointly with Test-Time Self-Distillation (TTSD), a parameter-efficient framework in which a frozen teacher, trained on complete modalities, guides an adaptive low-rank student via self-distillation, updating only a negligible number of parameters. Stability is built into this same loop through Fisher-Anchored Restoration (FAR), which monitors Fisher information stability to detect convergence versus drift and restores the student toward the teacher's anchor when distributional shifts are identified. Our experiments on MELD, DFEW, and BAH under 0%-50% missing modalities show that this unified adaptation-restoration design consistently outperforms entropy-based adaptation, RAG, and perplexity-based generation over long adaptation horizons, where baselines without restoration progressively degrade while TTSD-FAR remains consistent.

</details>


### [14] [Depth Anything V4: Dynamic 4D Scene Reconstruction via Riemannian Flow Matching on 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18388)
*Jiaming Fan,Jian Lu,Jinling Jia,Chenbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAV4是一个从单目视频进行动态4D场景重建的框架，核心创新是将黎曼流匹配(RFM)应用于4D高斯泼溅参数，直接在非欧几里得流形（尺度、旋转、不透明度）上定义概率路径，确保所有中间状态有效。通过受控实验隔离RFM贡献（+0.044 F-score增益），并提供了纠正后的计算成本分析与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态4D场景重建需要从单目视频中恢复场景的几何与动态信息，但现有方法在参数空间（如高斯泼溅的尺度、旋转、不透明度）上存在中间状态无效的问题，且缺乏对各组件贡献的清晰隔离分析。

Method: 1) 将黎曼流匹配(RFM)应用于4D高斯泼溅参数，在非欧几里得流形上定义概率路径；2) 通过受控实验设计（相同数据、架构和测试时优化），隔离RFM与TTO和预训练的独立贡献；3) 采用负高斯对数似然和期望校准误差进行不确定性量化。

Result: 确定性MLP基线F-score为0.762，RFM达到0.806，+0.044增益为RFM的独立贡献；预训练成本为360 GPU小时，在超过10,000场景的大规模部署下可摊销；DAV4在动态重建和新视角合成上优于先前Depth Anything模型及逐场景4D-GS，且无需人工标注的深度标签作为训练损失。

Conclusion: DAV4证明了RFM在4D高斯泼溅参数上的有效性，提供了清晰的组件贡献归因分析，展示了大规模部署的可行性，并在无人工深度标注条件下取得了超越现有方法的性能。

Abstract: We present Depth Anything V4 (DAV4), a framework for dynamic 4D scene reconstruction from monocular video. Our key contribution is the application of Riemannian Flow Matching (RFM) to 4D Gaussian Splatting parameters, defining probability paths directly on non-Euclidean manifolds (scale, rotation, opacity), ensuring all intermediate states are valid. Through controlled experiments, we isolate RFM's contribution from test-time optimization (TTO) and pre-training. A deterministic MLP baseline with the same data, architecture, and TTO achieves F-score 0.762; RFM achieves 0.806 - the +0.044 gain is RFM's isolated contribution. We provide corrected computational cost analysis: pre-training is 360 GPU-hours, amortizing for large-scale deployment (over 10,000 scenes). Uncertainty is quantified via Negative Gaussian Log-Likelihood and Expected Calibration Error. DAV4 outperforms prior Depth Anything models and per-scene 4D-GS on dynamic reconstruction and novel-view synthesis, while using no human-annotated depth labels as training losses.

</details>


### [15] [What Does Attention Transfer Transfer? Attention Structure and Robustness in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18399)
*Jesse Ponnock*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过系统实验证明，在ImageNet-100上，ViT-S蒸馏学生模型虽然能近乎完美地复制教师模型的注意力图（距离比微调低两个数量级且不随时间漂移），但其在分布偏移下的鲁棒性缺口主要是训练成熟度假象而非注意力结构缺陷。通过完成被早停中断的训练计划（在3个种子中的2个中低于预注册阈值），并对注意力结构进行直接干预（压缩跨行冗余）后发现无鲁棒性响应，结论是缺口源于特征而非可见的注意力结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已证明ViT复制教师注意力图能恢复大部分微调的分类精度，但在分布偏移下仍有可测量的差距。然而没有人直接测量过这种复制在注意力结构中到底传递了什么，以及如何与鲁棒性挂钩。作者希望构建这种仪器化测量，厘清注意力结构转移与鲁棒性差距之间的因果关系。

Method: 作者在ImageNet-100上训练ViT-S学生以复制自监督教师的注意力图，构建了三种仪器化手段：（1）直接测量蒸馏学生与教师注意力结构的距离（相对微调）；（2）沿时间轴追踪训练成熟度与鲁棒性差距的关系，完成被早停中断的训练计划；（3）直接干预注意力结构——将跨行冗余压缩至蒸馏与微调条件结构分离的一半，观察鲁棒性响应。实验规模为14倍更少参数、10倍更少数据。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）蒸馏学生的注意力转移近乎完全且永久——与教师距离比微调低约两个数量级，额外训练不漂移；（2）在小规模下鲁棒性差距真实存在但有时间轴——完成被早停中断的训练计划后，3个种子中2个低于预注册阈值，等精度比较给出相同结果，说明终点差距主要是训练成熟度假象；（3）直接干预注意力结构（压缩跨行冗余一半）在两种注册的精度匹配方式下均无可检测的鲁棒性响应。

Conclusion: 通过消除法加干预实验，作者结论是：在ImageNet-100/ViT-S这一规模范围内，蒸馏与微调之间的鲁棒性差距源于特征表示而非可见的注意力结构。注意力覆盖图展示的是模型"看哪里"而非"知道什么"。这一结论的范围限定在作者测量的具体规模与数据体制内。

Abstract: Vision transformers (ViTs) trained to copy a pretrained teacher's attention maps recover most of fine-tuning's in-distribution accuracy yet fall measurably short of it under distribution shift, as recent work has shown. What the copy delivers has never been measured directly in the attention structure and tied to robustness. We build that instrumentation for ViT-S students of a self-supervised teacher on ImageNet-100, and report three findings that triangulate one conclusion. First, the transfer is essentially perfect and permanently so: the distilled student's attention ends up roughly two orders of magnitude closer to the teacher's than fine-tuning does, and does not drift with additional training. Second, the gap is real at 14$\times$ fewer parameters and 10$\times$ less data than previously studied, but it has a time axis. It tracks training maturity, and completing the schedules that the stopping rule interrupted closes it below our pre-registered threshold in two of three seeds, with comparisons at equal accuracy giving the same result. The endpoint gap at this scale is substantially a training-maturity artifact: robustness matures later than accuracy, and stopping rules tuned to accuracy undersample it. Third, forcing cross-row redundancy down by half the structural separation between the distilled and fine-tuned conditions produces no detectable robustness response under two registered ways of matching accuracy. Verified transfer, a gap that closes while the structure never moves, and a null under direct intervention are together consistent with the deficit residing in features, not in the visible attention structure. This is elimination plus intervention, and its scope is the regime we measured. In this regime, attention overlays show where a model looks, not what it knows.

</details>


### [16] [JSL-DC: A Word-Level Japanese Sign Language Dataset with Linguist-Derived Descriptions for Distinguishing Confusable Signs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18412)
*Ken Takaki,Asuka Ando,Misa Suzuki,Uiko Yano,Masaya Tsujimoto,Bill Neubauer,Ananay Vikram Gupta,Rose Shao,Matthias Hoppe,Sahir Shahryar,Celeste Mason,Kai Kunze,Yohei Oseki,Yoshihiro Kawahara,Thad Starner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了JSL-DC，这是目前最大的日本手语（JSL）数据集，包含来自19位手语者的36.7K视频和270个JSL词汇。该数据集以聋人为中心设计，并提供语言学家的易混淆符号描述。基于这些描述启发的模型在易混淆子集上比SOTA方法提升9.8%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聋童的手语习得至关重要，但95%的聋童出生于听力正常的父母家庭，这些父母往往不精通手语。手语识别技术可以帮助父母与孩子交流，但日本手语（JSL）缺乏大规模、多手语者的数据集，阻碍了能够泛化到新用户的模型开发。

Method: 1) 构建JSL-DC数据集：包含36.7K视频、19位手语者、270个JSL词汇，是视频数量最大的JSL数据集。2) 采用Deaf-centric流程：词汇由聋人和Coda语言学家选择以促进亲子交流，所有参与者均为日常使用JSL的聋人，数据经过聋人语言学家的两阶段审查。3) 提供语言学家衍生的区分易混淆符号的描述。4) 基于这些描述设计识别模型。

Result: 提出的基于语言学描述启发的模型在易混淆子集上比最先进的手语识别方法提升了9.8%的准确率。

Conclusion: 数据集及语言学描述将以CC-BY 4.0许可发布，以加速手语识别研究，为解决JSL数据稀缺问题提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Effective sign language (SL) acquisition is crucial for deaf children, yet 95% are born to hearing parents who often lack proficiency in SL. SL recognition can power learning tools to help parents communicate with their children. However, Japanese Sign Language (JSL) lacks large-scale, multi-signer datasets, hindering the development of models that can generalize to new users. To address this gap, we introduce JSL-DC, the largest JSL dataset by video count, comprising 36.7K videos from 19 signers. The entire process was Deaf-centric: the lexicon comprising 270 JSL words was selected by Deaf and Coda linguists to facilitate parent-child communication, all participants were Deaf individuals who use JSL daily, and the data underwent a two-stage review process involving Deaf linguists. Moreover, we provide linguist-derived descriptions for distinguishing confusable signs. We demonstrate that the proposed model inspired by the descriptions outperforms state-of-the-art recognition methods by 9.8% on the confusable subset. The dataset, along with its linguistic description that inspires new models, will be released under a CC-BY 4.0 license to accelerate research in SL recognition.

</details>


### [17] [CoMVS-GS: Collaborative Multi-View Stereo and 3D Gaussian Splatting for Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18413)
*Shihan Chen,Junjing Zhang,Qingsong Yan,Haibing Liu,Haofan Ren,Fei Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoMVS-GS 是一个将多视角立体视觉（MVS）与3D高斯泼溅相结合的表面重建框架，通过MVS点初始化、PatchMatch-3DGS互监督和Delaunay图割网格提取，在弱观测和遮挡区域实现更准确、更紧凑的网格重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅在高效新视角合成方面表现出色，但在弱观测和遮挡区域的网格重建中，高斯原语可能生长为不稳定或几何不一致的结构，导致重建精度不足。

Method: 1) 从密集MVS点初始化高斯原语，具有预扁平化尺度和法线对齐方向，提供比稀疏SfM初始化更强的几何先验；2) 引入PatchMatch-3DGS互监督机制，高斯渲染的深度和法线初始化PatchMatch细化，而细化的PatchMatch深度监督高斯优化；3) 用Delaunay图割网格提取管线替代TSDF体素融合，降低对体素分辨率的敏感性。

Result: 在DTU、GauU-Scene V2和MatrixCity数据集上的实验表明，CoMVS-GS在物体级重建上保持竞争力，在室外场景中提升了几何精度和网格紧凑性，同时保持高渲染质量。

Conclusion: CoMVS-GS通过融合MVS与3D高斯泼溅的优势，有效解决了弱约束区域的几何重建问题，在保持渲染质量的同时提升了网格重建的准确性和紧凑性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting enables efficient novel view synthesis, but accurate mesh reconstruction remains difficult in weakly observed and occluded regions, where Gaussian primitives may grow into unstable or geometrically inconsistent structures. We propose CoMVS-GS, a general surface reconstruction framework that combines Multi-View Stereo with Gaussian splatting. CoMVS-GS initializes Gaussian primitives from dense multi-view stereo points with pre-flattened scales and normal-aligned orientations, providing stronger geometric priors than sparse structure-from-motion initialization and reducing ambiguity during early optimization. It further introduces PatchMatch-3DGS Mutual Supervision, where Gaussian-rendered depths and normals initialize PatchMatch refinement, and refined PatchMatch depths supervise Gaussian optimization to improve weakly constrained geometry. For surface extraction, CoMVS-GS replaces truncated signed distance field voxel fusion with a Delaunay graph-cut meshing pipeline, reducing sensitivity to voxel resolution while preserving visibility-consistent surface evidence. Experiments on DTU, GauU-Scene V2, and MatrixCity show that CoMVS-GS remains competitive on object-level reconstruction and improves geometric accuracy and mesh compactness in outdoor scenes while maintaining high rendering quality.

</details>


### [18] [COSTA: A Cluster-Centric Paradigm for Annotation-Free Open-Set Semantic Segmentation of Aerial Point Clouds with Domain Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18479)
*Yanghong Lin,Li Fang,Tianyu Li,Shudong Zhou,Wei Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COSTA提出了一种面向航空点云语义分割的测试时自适应方法，通过聚类中心化的开放集语义传播，使预训练模型能够在推理阶段适应目标域，并分割源标签空间之外的目标特定类别，无需额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空点云的语义分割在域偏移下面临泛化危机。现有测试时自适应方法受限于闭集标签假设和非可扩展的点级分割流程，无法处理语义偏移，更无法分割源标签空间之外的目标域特定类别。

Method: COSTA将闭集点级自适应转变为聚类中心化的开放集语义传播。首先通过成熟的测试时自适应桥接域差距，然后在适配后的特征空间中基于相似度分布将目标域点云分组为少量语义簇，最后通过簇级投票将开放词汇视觉-语言模型获得的高置信伪标签传播到所有点。

Result: 以DALES为源域，COSTA在三个具有不同域和异质类别空间的航空点云基准上实现了按需分割，在新设置下达到最高70.09%的mIoU。

Conclusion: 核心理念是：经过有效测试时适配后，航空点云的丰富特征分布可被蒸馏为紧凑且可分离的语义质心，且这些质心可跨标签空间迁移，从而实现了在显著域差距和混合语义偏移下的航空点云测试时自适应。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of aerial point cloud is trapped in a generalization crisis under distinct domain shifts. While test-time adaptation offers a privacy-preserving and computationally efficient way to adapt pre-trained models to unlabeled target-domain data during inference, existing methods, bound to closed-set label assumptions and non-scalable point-wise segmentation pipelines, still struggle with semantic shifts. We ask: can we adapt any given pre-trained aerial point cloud segmentation model to a shifted target domain at the inference phase alone, without additional training, while segmenting target-specific categories beyond the source label space on demand? This paper introduces COSTA, which breaks this limitation by shifting from closed-set point-wise adaptation to cluster-centric open-set semantic propagation. Our core discovery is that, once effectively adapted at test time, the rich feature distribution of aerial point clouds can be distilled into a compact set of well-separated semantic centroids that are transferable across label spaces. COSTA leverages this to reformulate open-set semantic segmentation as a cluster-level propagating process: it first bridges the domain gap through proven test-time adaptation, then groups each batch of target-domain points into a small set of semantic clusters based on the similarity distribution in the adapted feature space, and finally propagates high-confidence pseudo labels obtained from an open-vocabulary vision-language model to all points through cluster-level voting. This cluster-centric paradigm enables test-time adaptation of aerial point clouds under significant domain gaps with mixed semantic shifts. With DALES as the source domain, COSTA enables on-demand segmentation across three aerial point cloud benchmarks with distinct domains and heterogeneous category spaces, achieving up to 70.09% mIoU under this new setting.

</details>


### [19] [Partition the Support, Reconstruct the Residual: Training-Free Sparse Attention for Video Generation and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18484)
*Pardis Taghavi,Reza Langari,Gaurav Pandey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SparsePR是一种针对视频Transformer的训练无关块稀疏注意力加速方法，通过响应耦合分区（Response-Coupled Partitioning）和探针拟合残差重建（Probe-Fitted Residual Reconstruction）结合，在低执行密度下显著降低注意力重建误差并保持生成质量，实现1.48x-2.61x端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练无关的块稀疏注意力可以加速视频Transformer，但仅依赖行式注意力集中的路由选择并不足以构成可执行的稀疏算子。查询共享同一块路由时的支持集可能重叠度差，且仅保留注意力质量无法确定跳过交互带来的softmax后误差。文章指出分区几何既影响池化支持，也影响从稀疏输出预测剩余残差的可预测性，因此需要同时优化分区策略与残差重建。

Method: SparsePR包含两个核心组件：(1) Response-Coupled Partitioning——利用采样查询的键响应形成配对的K/V组，其质心诱导查询-响应坐标用于共享路由，以改善分区几何；(2) Probe-Fitted Residual Reconstruction——用一小部分精确查询行校准一个按调用区分的仿射校正项，从稀疏输出在探针残差观测到的输出子空间内重建被跳过的注意力贡献。

Result: 在四个异构视频生成与世界模型上，SparsePR持续降低注意力重建误差。消融实验表明探针拟合贡献了大部分误差降低，而响应耦合分区降低了硬丢弃误差并改善了有限探针预算下的重建效果。在22.0%-26.0%的实际执行对密度下保持生成质量，实现1.48x-2.61x端到端加速。

Conclusion: 分区几何与残差可预测性共同决定稀疏注意力的效果，仅靠路由选择或注意力质量不足以支撑可执行的稀疏算子。SparsePR通过响应耦合分区与探针拟合残差重建的组合，在保证生成质量的同时显著提升视频Transformer推理速度，为训练无关稀疏注意力提供了更完整且可执行的方案。

Abstract: Training-free block-sparse attention can accelerate video transformers, but row-wise attention concentration does not by itself specify an executable sparse operator. Queries sharing a block route may have poorly overlapping supports, while retained attention mass alone does not determine the post-softmax error from skipped interactions. We show that partition geometry affects both pooled support and the predictability of the remaining residual from the sparse output. We introduce SparsePR, which combines Response-Coupled Partitioning with Probe-Fitted Residual Reconstruction. Sampled-query key responses form paired K/V groups, whose centroids induce query-response coordinates for shared routing. A small set of exact query rows then calibrates a call-specific affine correction from the sparse output within the output subspace observed in the probe residuals. Across four heterogeneous video generation and world models, SparsePR consistently reduces attention-reconstruction error. Ablations show that probe fitting accounts for most of this reduction, while response-coupled partitioning lowers hard-drop error and improves reconstruction under a finite probe budget. SparsePR preserves generation quality at 22.0-26.0% realized executed-pair density while achieving 1.48x-2.61x end-to-end speedups. Project page: https://pardistaghavi.github.io/SparsePR-website/

</details>


### [20] [DyG$^2$T: Modeling Object Dynamics with 3D Gaussian Temporal-Spatial Particle Graph Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18498)
*Yansong Wang,Zhaobo Qi,Xinyan Liu,Beichen Zhang,Shuhui Wang,Weigang Zhang,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DyG²T框架，通过空间上补全关键点局部细节、时间上增强帧间判别性、以及基于粒子图的全局注意力建模多尺度交互，实现对从有限视觉观测中物体动力学的准确轨迹预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力学建模方法将粒子表示压缩为稀疏关键点并进行局部约束的交互建模，丢弃了细粒度局部细节，且跨空间和时间尺度的判别性交互建模不清晰，导致轨迹漂移和外观预测不准确。

Method: DyG²T包含三部分：1) 空间上，通过聚合邻域原始粒子位置补全关键点的细粒度局部细节，并显式编码关键点间相对偏移增强几何结构感知；2) 时间上，引入时间解缠网络(TDN)在隐空间识别主导跨帧变化并放大帧间差异，再通过时间注意力聚合捕捉帧级时间演化线索；3) 交互建模上，使用粒子图Transformer的全局注意力保留关键点间长程判别性依赖，缓解局部约束建模导致的表征同质化。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，DyG²T实现了准确的动力学建模与推理，并展现出较强的跨物体和真实世界泛化能力。

Conclusion: DyG²T通过空间补全和时间判别双管齐下，结合全局粒子图交互建模，有效解决了现有方法在细粒度细节丢失和局部建模局限上的问题，为物体运动轨迹预测提供了更稳健的方案。

Abstract: Modeling object dynamics from limited visual observations is a fundamental problem for enabling accurate motion trajectory prediction in embodied interaction scenarios. Existing dynamics modeling methods first compress reconstructed particle representations into sparse Key Points and model their evolution using locally constrained interactions, thereby discarding fine-grained local details and obscuring discriminative interaction modeling across spatial and temporal scales, leading to drifting trajectories and inaccurate appearance prediction. To tackle these issues, we propose DyG$^2$T, a dynamics modeling framework that infers object motion trajectories by spatially completing and temporally discriminating Key Point representations and modeling multi-scale interaction over particle graphs. Spatially, DyG$^2$T enriches each Key Point by aggregating neighboring raw particle positions to recover fine-grained local details, while explicitly encoding relative offsets among Key Points to enhance geometric structure perception. Temporally, we introduce a Temporal Disentangling Network (TDN) to identify dominant cross-frame variations in latent space and amplify inter-frame differences, yielding temporally discriminative representations that are subsequently aggregated via Temporal Attention to capture frame-wise temporal evolution cues. For comprehensive interaction modeling, a Particle Graph Transformer leverages global attention to preserve discriminative long-range dependencies among Key Points, mitigating representation homogenization induced by locality-constrained modeling and providing a robust basis for accurate trajectory prediction. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that DyG$^2$T achieves accurate dynamics modeling and reasoning, and exhibits strong cross-object and real-world generalization.

</details>


### [21] [Cross-Modal MRI Ovary Segmentation in Endometriosis Using Unpaired TVUS Prototype Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18515)
*Xingjian Kang,Lina Felsner,Dominik Perrin,Daiqi Liu,Jasmin Arjomandi,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich,Alexandra Stoll,Katharina Breininger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双分支框架，用于在经阴道超声（TVUS）和磁共振成像（MRI）之间进行跨模态卵巢分割。通过将MedSAM3与TVUS导出的原型库相结合，对齐两种模态间解剖一致的特征表示。实验表明，与多种SOTA方法相比，该方法在定量和定性上均提升超过5个百分点，消融实验验证了原型库和源域TVUS预训练的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TVUS和MRI为子宫内膜异位症图像分析提供互补信息，但现有研究主要集中于单模态分析或疾病分类，跨模态卵巢分割领域尚未充分探索。此外，MRI中卵巢目标尺寸小、与周围盆腔结构边界模糊，导致分割难度显著增加。

Method: 提出一个双分支框架，核心思路是适配MedSAM3并结合TVUS导出的原型库（prototype bank），以对齐两种模态间解剖一致的特征表示，从而实现跨TVUS和MRI的卵巢分割。

Result: 在子宫内膜异位症相关的TVUS和MRI数据集上进行大量实验，与多种SOTA方法相比，所提出的双分支方法在定量和定性指标上均提升超过5个百分点。消融实验验证了原型库等组件的贡献，并证实源域TVUS域中预热预训练的重要性。

Conclusion: 双分支框架结合原型对齐策略能有效解决跨模态卵巢分割难题，显著优于现有方法，为多模态子宫内膜异位症图像分析提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Transvaginal ultrasound (TVUS) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) provide complementary information for endometriosis image analysis, yet existing studies mainly focus on single-modality analysis or disease classification, leaving cross-modal ovarian segmentation largely unexplored. In this work, to tackle the increased difficulty of ovary segmentation in MRI due to ovaries' small target size and ambiguous boundaries with surrounding pelvic structures, we propose a dual branch framework for ovary segmentation across TVUS and MRI. More specifically, by adapting MedSAM3 with TVUS-derived prototype bank, we aim to align anatomically consistent feature representations across both modalities. Extensive experiments are conducted on endometriosis-related TVUS and MRI datasets. We observe quantitative and qualitative improvements of over 5 percentage points for the proposed dual-branch approach compared with multiple state-of-the-art methods. Furthermore, our ablation study shows the contribution of individual components such as the prototype bank and the importance of warm-up pretraining in the source TVUS domain.

</details>


### [22] [OptiModNet: A UNet-Transformer Hybrid with Grouped-Query and Channel Attention for Optic Disc and Cup Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18516)
*Soumili Ghosh,Debapriya Roy,Aryan Das,Bikash Santra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OptiModNet，一种轻量级混合架构用于视盘和视杯的精确分割，在REFUGE2数据集上达到SOTA性能（超过现有方法2.5%以上），仅需3.73 GFLOPs和1.93M参数，兼顾高性能与低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青光眼的早期检测需要精确的视盘和视杯分割，但现有深度学习方法（UNet、ViT、Diffusion等）虽性能优异却计算开销大。UNet缺乏全局上下文建模，ViT计算密集，混合架构如UNetR虽提升性能但增加复杂度。在资源受限的临床环境中，需要低计算、高精度的方法以支持大规模快速筛查。

Method: OptiModNet是一种轻量级混合架构，在网络的多个阶段集成多种注意力机制，同时增强局部和全局特征表示。此外，引入聚合金字塔损失（Aggregated Pyramid Loss），在多解码器深度监督预测，以促进更好的梯度流动和结构一致性。

Result: 在REFUGE2数据集上对视盘和视杯分割任务进行评估，达到SOTA性能，超过现有方法2.5%以上，同时保持高效率，仅需3.73 GFLOPs和1.93M参数。

Conclusion: OptiModNet证明了在保持极低计算资源需求的同时实现高性能视盘/视杯分割是可行的，为大规模青光眼筛查和资源受限临床环境中的部署提供了有效解决方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Precise segmentation of the optic disc and cup is critical for the early detection and diagnosis of glaucoma. However, achieving consistently high performance across datasets while maintaining low computational requirements remains a significant challenge. In glaucoma detection, low-computation methods are crucial for enabling rapid, large-scale screening and facilitating deployment in resource-limited clinical environments. While deep learning models such as UNets, Vision Transformers (ViTs), and Diffusion models have demonstrated strong segmentation performance but these methods often come with substantial computational overhead. UNets are efficient at capturing local features but are limited in modeling global contextual information. Conversely, ViTs excel at long-range dependency modeling but are computationally intensive. Hybrid architectures, such as UNetR, which combine transformer-based encoders with UNet-style decoders, have shown improved performance but while incurring additional complexity. Considering these, in this work, we propose OptiModNet, a light weight novel hybrid architecture tailored for optic disc and cup segmentation. The model integrates diverse attention mechanisms at multiple stages of the network to enhance both local and global feature representation. We include an Aggregated Pyramid Loss that supervises predictions at multiple decoder depths, to promote better gradient flow and structural consistency. We evaluate OptiModNet on the REFUGE2 dataset for both optic disc and cup segmentation tasks. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, exceeding existing approaches by over 2.5\%, while maintaining high efficiency with only 3.73 GFLOPs and 1.93M parameters. The code is available at https://github.com/SG1947/OptiModNet.

</details>


### [23] [Prior-Conditioned Gaussian Discriminants for Generalizable AI-generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18523)
*Shashank Kotyan,Makoto Shing,Yuki Imajuku,Rujikorn Charakorn,Tarin Clanuwat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究AI生成图像检测作为迁移系统（由训练先验、冻结编码器特征空间和决策规则描述），提出先验条件高斯判别梯形分类器（基于一阶和二阶特征统计量的闭式分类头），在Percept-Lens协议（39个公开数据集、710万图像）上验证，发现最佳梯级在对齐先验和编码器条件下常能与甚至超越已发布的检测器分类头，并量化了训练先验敏感性、基于矩分类头的数据效率以及高斯移位度量的表征依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成器使合成图像无处不在，但检测器在生成器、提示/风格、源域同时变化时常常失效，现有检测方法的跨域迁移鲁棒性不足，缺乏系统性的分析框架。

Method: 将AI生成图像检测建模为迁移系统（训练先验、冻结编码器特征空间、决策规则），提出先验条件高斯判别梯形分类器，即基于一阶和二阶特征统计量在嵌套协方差假设下构建的闭式分类头；构建Percept-Lens统一协议，覆盖39个公开数据集（710万图像）进行系统评估。

Result: 在Percept-Lens协议上，最佳梯级在先验和编码器匹配时经常与已发布的AIGI检测器分类头竞争，有时超越；同时量化了训练先验的强敏感性、基于矩分类头的数据效率和高斯移位度量的表征依赖性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明需要更细粒度的（先验、编码器、分类头）级别报告以及更强的分析基线来评估AIGI的跨域迁移，为AI生成图像检测提供了系统化的分析框架和基准。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generators have made synthetic images ubiquitous, but detectors often fail under simultaneous shifts in generator, prompt/style, and source-domain. We study AI-generated image detection as a transfer system described by training prior, frozen encoder feature space, and decision rule, and ask when classifier head training adds value beyond what is already separable in modern features. As a controlled diagnostic, we fit a prior-conditioned Gaussian discriminant ladder: closed-form heads built from first- and second-order feature statistics under nested covariance assumptions. On Percept-Lens, a unified protocol over 39 public datasets (7.1 million images), the best rung is frequently competitive with, and sometimes exceeds, released AI-generated image detector heads when matched on both prior and encoder. We further quantify strong sensitivity to the training prior, data-efficiency of moment-based heads, and representation dependence of Gaussian shift metrics, motivating (prior, encoder, head)-level reporting and stronger analytical baselines for AIGI transfer.

</details>


### [24] [StateTrace: An Object-Centric Framework for Hidden-State Spatiotemporal Reasoning in Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18532)
*Yu Han,Wenhao Li,Yichao Cao,Hongyan Xu,Shuo Yang,Shan You,Xiu Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StateTrace框架，解决视频大语言模型在长视频中目标对象消失时的隐藏状态时空推理问题，并构建了HSR-Bench基准，显著提升多个VideoLLM的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型在目标对象不可见时，常常将"不可见"误判为"未知"，无法进行长视频中的隐藏状态时空推理，即从上下文交互推断对象在长时间不可见期间的状态变化。

Method: 提出StateTrace对象中心框架，构建可复用的时空状态记忆，组织对象轨迹、对象间关系和状态转换事件为结构化推理基底；推理时检索与问题相关的状态演化轨迹并转换为紧凑推理线索，使模型能显式推理对象消失原因、不可见期间状态演化及查询时刻状态是否应持续。

Result: 构建HSR-Bench基准（1,427个视频QA样本，1,384个视频），在多个VideoLLM上验证StateTrace持续提升公开基准和HSR-Bench的性能，例如将VideoLLaMA3从39.6提升至64.2。

Conclusion: StateTrace通过显式的隐藏状态推理机制有效解决了长视频中对象不可见时的推理难题，验证了结构化时空状态记忆在增强视频理解模型推理能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Existing VLMs have achieved strong performance in video understanding, yet they struggle with long-video spatiotemporal reasoning when target objects become invisible, often mistaking "invisible" for "unknown". We define this challenge as hidden-state spatiotemporal reasoning: inferring object states during prolonged invisible intervals from context interactions. To address this, we propose StateTrace, a novel object-centric framework that endows VideoLLMs with an explicit mechanism for hidden state reasoning in long videos. StateTrace builds a reusable spatiotemporal state memory that organizes object trajectories, inter-object relations, and state-transition events into a structured reasoning substrate. At inference time, it retrieves question-relevant state-evolution trajectories and converts them into compact reasoning cues, enabling the model to explicitly reason about why an object disappears, how its state evolves while invisible, and whether that state should persist at query time. We further build HSR-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark for hidden-state reasoning, containing 1,427 video-QA samples from 1,384 unique videos. Extensive experiments across multiple VideoLLMs show that StateTrace consistently improves performance on both public benchmarks and HSR-Bench (e.g., improving VideoLLaMA3 from 39.6 to 64.2 on HSR-Bench).

</details>


### [25] [Zero-Shot SAM2 Segmentation and Vision Transformer-Based Recognition of Elamite Cuneiform Symbols from Degraded Tablet Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18544)
*Utsav Poudel,Rasik Bhattarai,Siddhartha Pathak,Raghavendra Ramacharna,Gaurav Jaswal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EpigraphNet，一种由分割引导的Transformer流水线，用于自动识别古代楔形文字。它通过亮度自适应形态学预处理和SAM2-Large零样本分割生成干净的二进制符号掩码，再由带逆频率类加权的Vision Transformer（ViT-B/16）进行分类，在波斯波利斯要塞档案数据集上达到86.41%的top-1准确率，相比最强CNN基线提升17.21个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古代楔形文字的自动识别面临复合信号退化问题：黏土板三维浮雕导致空间变化光照和投射阴影、表面侵蚀引入与真实符号印记重叠的结构化噪声、以及141个符号类别间的严重类不平衡损害分类器可靠性。

Method: 采用分割引导的Transformer流水线：(1) 亮度自适应形态学预处理；(2) 零样本SAM2-Large分割生成干净二元符号掩码；(3) 使用带逆频率类加权的微调ViT-B/16进行分类。整个流水线在NVIDIA A100上每个符号约18ms。

Result: 在132类基准上达到86.41%的top-1准确率，比最强CNN基线（ResNet-101，69.20%）提升17.21个百分点，比四个现代骨干网络（DeiT-B/16、Swin-B、ConvNeXt-B、EfficientNet-B4）在相同条件下提升5.31-12.91%。更低的Spearman相关性表明在频繁和稀有类别间的识别更加均衡。

Conclusion: 分割引导（SAM2）与视觉Transformer相结合能有效解决楔形文字识别的多源退化问题，不仅显著提升识别精度，还改善了类别不平衡情况下的识别均衡性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated recognition of ancient cuneiform script poses a compound signal-degradation problem: the three-dimensional relief of clay tablets creates spatially varying illumination and cast shadows, surface erosion introduces structured noise that overlaps with genuine sign impressions, and severe class imbalance across 141 sign categories undermines classifier reliability. We introduce EpigraphNet, a segmentation-guided transformer pipeline evaluated on the Persepolis Fortification Archive. From 1,239 annotated tablet images, brightness-adaptive morphological preprocessing and zero-shot SAM2-Large segmentation generate clean binary symbol masks, which a fine-tuned Vision Transformer (ViT-B/16) with inverse-frequency class weighting then classifies. EpigraphNet reaches 86.41% top-1 accuracy on a 132-class benchmark, a 17.21 percentage-point gain over the strongest CNN baseline (ResNet-101, 69.20%) and 5.31-12.91% over four modern backbones (DeiT-B/16, Swin-B, ConvNeXt-B, EfficientNet-B4) under identical conditions. The full pipeline runs at approximately 18 ms per sign on an NVIDIA A100 GPU. A lower Spearman correlation between sign frequency and per-class performance indicates more balanced recognition across frequent and rare classes. Implementation is available at: github.com/r11up/sam-guided-vit

</details>


### [26] [PATE-Forensics: Perception-as-Tool for Explainable Deepfake Forensics with General-Purpose MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18573)
*Yaqi Li,Jielun Peng,Yabin Wang,Jincheng Liu,Xiaopeng Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PATE-Forensics，采用"感知即工具"(Perception-as-Tool)范式，将检测与定位架构性地解耦于解释生成之外，同时将检测与定位紧密耦合为一个法医感知工具。基于DINOv3的工具结合多粒度检测模块和线索引导定位模块，将输出结构化上下文提供给通用MLLM生成解释，无需任务特定微调。在DDL-X Track 3上取得0.89的最佳官方分数，领先第二名0.19分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释深度伪造取证方法通常依赖任务适配的MLLM同时处理检测、定位和解释三个任务，但这种联合方式可能导致各任务间相互干扰。受智能体工具使用范式的启发，作者希望将感知（检测与定位）与解释生成解耦，让专用工具负责感知，通用MLLM专注于解释，从而提升各环节的性能与灵活性。

Method: 提出Perception-as-Tool范式并实例化为PATE-Forensics：(1) 基于DINOv3的法医感知工具，包含多粒度检测模块（整合全局、patch级和segment级证据）与线索引导定位模块（将patch和segment级证据空间化为伪造分数图指导密集掩码预测）；(2) 工具产生的原始图像与法医感知输出构成结构化法医上下文；(3) 通用MLLM在提示约束引导下生成解释，无需任务特定微调。

Result: 在DDL-X Track 3上，PATE-Forensics获得最佳官方分数0.89，超过第二名团队0.19分，验证了该架构在深度伪造检测、定位与解释联合任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: 感知与解释的解耦架构（Perception-as-Tool范式）为可解释深度伪造取证提供了一种高效且可扩展的新思路，利用通用MLLM结合专用感知工具即可实现强性能，避免了任务特定微调的成本，代码已开源。

Abstract: Existing explainable deepfake forensic methods typically rely on task-adapted MLLM to jointly address detection, localization, and explanation. Inspired by agent-style tool use, we instead introduce a Perception-as-Tool paradigm and instantiate it as PATE-Forensics, which architecturally decouples detection and localization from explanation generation while coupling detection and localization as tightly as possible within a forensic perception tool. The DINOv3-based tool couples a multi-granularity detection module that integrates global, patch-level, and segment-level evidence with a cue-guided localization module by spatializing the patch-level and segment-level evidence into forgery score maps that guide dense mask prediction. The original image and forensic perception outputs produced by the tool form structured forensic context for a general-purpose MLLM, which is guided by prompt constraints to generate explanations without task-specific fine-tuning. On DDL-X Track 3, PATE-Forensics achieves the best official score of 0.89, outperforming the second-ranked team by 0.19 points. Our code is available at https://github.com/yqli00000/PATE-Forensics.

</details>


### [27] [MR-IQA-2: Faithful Image Quality Reflection via Fine-Grained Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18579)
*Yuan li,Youyuan Lin,Chenhui Chu,Shin'ya Nishida*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MR-IQA-2框架，这是一个用于盲图像质量评估的actor-editor-judge架构，通过推理-编辑-反思机制解耦推理与评分的信用分配，并提供可验证的监督以实现更忠实的质量推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在IQA中通过人类评分监督推理，但评分准确性不保证推理的忠实性；共享奖励会模糊监督来源，可能在评分碰巧正确时强化不忠实的推理。因此需要解耦推理与评分的信用分配，并为忠实推理提供可验证的监督。

Method: 提出MR-IQA-2框架，包含三个组件：actor生成图像质量推理，editor根据识别出的质量因素修改图像，frozen judge对比原始与编辑后图像为actor的推理提供反思性监督。采用细粒度信用分配解耦推理与评分监督：judge反馈监督推理，人类评分监督预测评分，通过掩码token特定更新区分两类信号，同时保持推理到评分的因果关系。

Result: 在IQA基准上，MR-IQA-2实现了与人类评分具有竞争力的对齐效果。视觉反思还能提供超越评分的更丰富、更忠实的视觉理解。

Conclusion: MR-IQA-2通过actor-editor-judge框架实现了推理与评分的解耦监督，提升了盲IQA中推理的忠实性和可靠性，其方法可能对图像质量优化及相关下游任务有启发意义。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for image quality assessment (IQA) by improving consistency between quality ratings and their underlying reasoning. However, most approaches supervise reasoning through human-provided ratings and rarely examine whether it faithfully reflects image quality. Rating accuracy alone does not ensure faithful reasoning; a shared reward also obscures supervision sources and may reinforce unfaithful reasoning when a correct rating occurs by chance. To improve the faithfulness and reliability of blind IQA, we aim to (1) decouple credit assignment for reasoning and rating and (2) provide verifiable supervision for faithful reasoning. We introduce MR-IQA-2, an actor-editor-judge framework that operationalizes reasoning-editing-reflection. The actor generates quality reasoning for an input image, and the editor revises the image according to the identified quality factors. A frozen judge compares the original and edited images and provides reflective supervision for the actor's reasoning. MR-IQA-2 further uses fine-grained credit assignment to decouple reasoning and rating supervision. Judge feedback supervises reasoning, whereas human ratings supervise the predicted rating. Masked token-specific updates distinguish these signals while preserving the causal relation from reasoning to rating. Across IQA benchmarks, MR-IQA-2 achieves competitive rating alignment with humans. Visual reflection also enables richer and more faithful visual understanding beyond rating, which may inform image-quality optimization and related downstream tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/RobinY99/MR-IQA-2.

</details>


### [28] [SPARC: Subspace Position-Aware Robust Few-Shot Calibration for Distribution-Shifted Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18585)
*Seokhee Han,Seungjun Chu,Mateusz Nowak,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPARC是一种少样本校准方法，通过拦截编码器与检测器之间的patch特征，并在每个空间单元格上执行子空间投影来消除部署时的分布偏移（如光照、夹具位置、传感器差异等），仅需k≤8张已验证正常的图像即可校正各种基于视觉的工业异常检测器，无需梯度或权重更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉的工业异常检测器通常在单一分布上校准，但实际部署时可能遇到光照、夹具位置或传感器特性变化，导致检测性能急剧下降。虽然针对当前批次进行自适应是自然方案，但标记异常样本稀缺，无法依赖大量标注。现有方法需要反向传播、检测器特定调优，或要求选择特征方向（这在少样本情况下无法合理确定）。因此需要一个仅使用少量已验证正常图像即可完成的通用校准方案。

Method: SPARC通过拦截编码器与检测器之间的patch特征，计算一个闭式、空间索引的部署期干扰估计，然后在每个单元格进行子空间投影来移除该干扰。它利用代数饱和秩r=k-1（k为正常图像数量，k≤8），在编码器的原始patch网格上操作，无需梯度或权重更新，可适配记忆库、密度、原型和互信息四类检测器。

Result: 在存在分布偏移的基准上，SPARC使所有7个检测器（其图像分数依赖于校正后的patch特征）的池化Image AUROC平均提升+13.8个百分点，AU-PRO_0.3提升+3.5个百分点；在无工程化偏移的基准上，变化很小且方向混合。对照实验表明增益来自逐单元格子空间结构而非图像本身。消融研究支持饱和秩选择，并刻画了对骨干网络和校准条件的敏感性。

Conclusion: SPARC提供了一种无需训练、无需检测器特定调整的通用少样本校准方案，能够显著提升在分布偏移下的工业异常检测性能，并通过逐单元格子空间投影这一关键结构设计实现了对多种检测器架构的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Vision-based industrial anomaly detectors are calibrated on one distribution but may be deployed on another that differs in illumination, fixture placement, or sensor characteristics, sharply degrading an otherwise accurate detector. Adapting to the incoming lot is a natural response, but labeled anomalies are scarce. We therefore consider calibration using only a handful of verified-normal images available before scoring the rest of the lot. Existing fixes require backpropagation, detector-specific tuning, or choices about feature directions that few calibration samples cannot justify. We present SPARC, a few-shot calibration method that intercepts patch features between encoder and detector and removes a closed-form, spatially indexed estimate of deployment-time nuisance through per-cell subspace projection. It needs only $k \le 8$ verified-normal images and uses the algebraic saturation rank $r{=}k{-}1$ on the encoder's native patch grid. The correction requires no gradient or weight updates and works with memory-bank, density, prototype, and mutual detectors. On the shift-prone benchmarks, SPARC improves pooled Image AUROC and AU-PRO$_{0.3}$ for all seven detectors whose image scores depend on corrected patch features by $+13.8$ and $+3.5$ percentage points (pp), respectively; on benchmarks without engineered shift, the changes are small and mixed. Controls that give competing corrections the same calibration images attribute these gains to the per-cell subspace structure rather than the images alone. Further ablations support the saturation-rank choice and characterize sensitivity to backbone and calibration conditions.

</details>


### [29] [OmniHandwritingOCR: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal LLMs in Handwritten OCR Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18586)
*Zinuo Guo,Min Zhang,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniHandwritingOCR，一个用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在手写OCR场景中表现的诊断性基准。该基准涵盖手写文本识别和手写数学表达式识别两个任务，包含6个子任务、12个子集和77.57K张标注图像。评测了13个开源和闭源系统，发现当前系统在手写识别上仍远未达到忠实转录水平，尤其在复杂多行公式上表现急剧下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCR基准大多集中在印刷文本或简单的单行输入上，缺乏对真实手写场景（如多语言手写、书写者错误、结构复杂的数学表达式）的充分覆盖。MLLMs被越来越多地用作OCR系统，但它们在实读手写方面的忠实性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 构建OmniHandwritingOCR基准：1) 覆盖手写文本识别（HTR）和手写数学表达式识别（HMER）两大任务；2) 包含6个子任务和12个子集，共77.57K标注图像，来源涵盖公开数据集和新收集的学生手写内容；3) 设计难度分层的多行公式语料库以测试结构复杂度增加的鲁棒性；4) 在统一协议下使用5种互补指标评估13个开源和闭源系统。

Result: 实验表明：当前系统在复杂多行公式上的性能急剧下降；模型排名在不同语言和公式设定下不一致；多个生成式模型出现幻觉现象，产生看似合理但视觉上无支撑的纠正内容。系统在手写OCR场景中仍远未实现忠实转录。

Conclusion: OmniHandwritingOCR为诊断多模态模型在手写OCR场景中的语言、内容、结构和视觉接地失败模式提供了具有挑战性的测试平台，揭示当前MLLMs在真实手写识别方面仍存在显著差距。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used as OCR systems in document and knowledge-processing pipelines, but their ability to faithfully read real handwriting remains underexplored. Existing OCR benchmarks focus largely on printed text or clean single-line inputs, leaving limited coverage of realistic handwritten OCR scenarios such as multilingual handwriting, writer errors, and structurally complex mathematical expressions. We introduce OmniHandwritingOCR, a diagnostic benchmark for evaluating MLLMs and OCR systems on handwritten OCR. It covers handwritten text recognition and handwritten mathematical expression recognition across six subtasks and twelve subsets, totaling 77.57K labeled images from public datasets and newly collected student writings. A key component is a difficulty-stratified multi-line formula corpus designed to test robustness under increasing structural complexity. We evaluate thirteen open- and closed-source systems with five complementary metrics under a unified protocol. Results show that current systems remain far from faithful transcription: performance drops sharply on complex multi-line formulas, model rankings vary across language and formula settings, and several generative models hallucinate plausible but visually unsupported corrections. OmniHandwritingOCR provides a challenging testbed for diagnosing language, content, structural, and visual-grounding failure modes of multimodal models in handwritten OCR scenarios.

</details>


### [30] [FD-CanKD: Frequency-Decoupled Cross-Attention Distillation as a Refinement Prior for Compact Object Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18590)
*YoungJae Cheong,Jhonghyun An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FD-CanKD（频率解耦交叉注意力知识蒸馏）框架，面向紧凑型目标检测器，从头部预测监督、关系级非局部上下文迁移和频率级组件选择性对齐三个互补层面传递教师知识。在COCO数据集上，固定50轮从头训练的对比实验中表现与代表性蒸馏基线相当；继续微调20轮后达到48.87 mAP50:95、65.84 mAP50和53.40 mAP75，且部署时移除所有蒸馏模块，学生网络仅19.7M参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型目标检测器适合资源受限场景，但表示能力有限，与大型模型存在精度差距。现有检测器蒸馏方法通常依赖预测级监督或单一特征对齐目标（响应、分布、相关性或频域匹配），缺乏多层级互补的知识迁移，导致紧凑学生模型的精度提升受限。

Method: FD-CanKD框架包含三个互补的蒸馏层级：(1) 头部级预测监督；(2) 关系级非局部上下文迁移——学生特征通过交叉注意力聚合教师侧空间上下文；(3) 频率级组件选择性对齐——保留互补的结构和细节敏感信息。在YOLOv12教师-学生设置下进行受控实验，训练后所有蒸馏模块可被移除。

Result: 在COCO数据集上，固定50轮从头训练的对比实验中FD-CanKD与代表性检测器蒸馏基线保持竞争力。继续微调20轮后达到48.87 mAP50:95、65.84 mAP50和53.40 mAP75，优于仅检测器微调的细化准备金学生。部署时学生网络仅19.7M参数。

Conclusion: FD-CanKD通过多层级互补蒸馏（预测+关系+频率）有效提升了紧凑检测器的精度，同时保证了部署时零额外开销。该框架在YOLOv12紧凑检测器场景中验证了其有效性，为资源受限视觉感知提供了实用的蒸馏解决方案。

Abstract: Compact object detectors are suitable for resource-constrained visual perception, but their limited representation capacity creates an accuracy gap relative to large models. Conventional detector distillation often relies on prediction-level supervision or a single feature-alignment target, such as response, distribution, correlation, or frequency-domain matching. Frequency-Decoupled Cross-Attention Knowledge Distillation (FD-CanKD) is presented as a detector-oriented framework that transfers teacher knowledge at three complementary levels: head-level prediction supervision, relation-level non-local context transfer, and frequency-level component-selective alignment. Student features first aggregate teacher-side spatial context through cross-attention-based relation transfer, after which frequency-aware alignment preserves complementary structural and detail-sensitive cues. Under controlled Microsoft Common Objects in Context (COCO) experiments, fixed 50-epoch from-scratch comparisons show that FD-CanKD remains competitive with representative detector knowledge distillation baselines. Post-distillation continued fine-tuning further produces a stronger refinement-ready student than detector-only fine-tuning, reaching 48.87 mean average precision (mAP) at intersection-over-union thresholds from 0.50 to 0.95 (mAP50:95), 65.84 mAP50, and 53.40 mAP75 after 20 additional epochs. All distillation modules are removed after training, leaving the deployed student unchanged at 19.7M parameters. The framework is instantiated and evaluated in a controlled YOLOv12 teacher-student setting as a representative compact-detector case study.

</details>


### [31] [ReX-Shot: Single-Image Rephotography via Geometry- and Camera-Grounded Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18593)
*Ruiqi Zhang,Hao Zhu,Wenhao Zhang,Qi Zhang,Junqi Shi,Ming Lu,Xun Cao,Zhan Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReX-Shot是一个统一的生成框架，首次实现从单张参考图像联合控制视角、焦距和参数化摄影效果（即单图重摄影）。它通过隐式变换的基础模型特征减少几何误差带来的投影偏差，并将焦距放大建模为几何引导的超分辨率问题，同时将摄影效果控制从2D提升到3D感知的外观编辑，实现跨视角和焦距的内容一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图重摄影方法将视角、焦距和摄影效果视为独立因素分开处理，在联合控制下表现不佳：新视角合成在焦距变化下会产生几何畸变，超分辨率和指令编辑局限于2D，无法可靠地扩展到新视角。根本原因在于单图像3D重建不完善以及连续焦距放大的采样极限。

Method: 1) 使用隐式变换的基础模型特征进行鲁棒的目标视角引导，降低几何误差导致的投影偏差；2) 将焦距放大形式化为几何引导的超分辨率问题，利用生成式细节先验恢复稀疏3D重采样过程中丢失的细节；3) 在3D感知生成骨干网络上，将摄影效果控制从2D滤波提升为3D感知的外观编辑，保持跨视角和焦距的内容一致性。

Result: 在视角、焦距和摄影效果三种控制任务上均优于代表性基线方法，并支持近实时的交互式重摄影。

Conclusion: ReX-Shot是首个将视角、焦距和参数化摄影效果统一在单一框架中联合控制的单图重摄影方法，证明了几何与相机约束下的生成框架能够实现一致且可控的图像重现。

Abstract: Single-image rephotography aims to synthesize new shots of a scene from a single reference image with specified viewpoints, focal lengths, and photographic effects, which are intrinsically coupled in imaging. Existing methods typically treat these factors separately and struggle under joint control: novel-view synthesis may introduce geometric distortions under focal-length changes, while super-resolution and instruction-guided editing remain confined to 2D and cannot reliably extend detail restoration or appearance control to novel viewpoints. We attribute these limitations to imperfect single-image 3D reconstruction and the sampling limit of continuous focal-length enlargement. To reduce projection bias from geometric errors, we use implicitly transformed foundation-model features for robust target-view guidance. We further formulate focal-length enlargement as a geometry-guided super-resolution problem and exploit generative detail priors to recover details lost during sparse 3D resampling. Built on this 3D-aware generative backbone, we lift photographic-effect control from 2D filtering to 3D-aware appearance editing, preserving content consistency across viewpoints and focal lengths. These components form ReX-Shot, a geometry- and camera-grounded generative framework for single-image rephotography. To our knowledge, ReX-Shot is the first unified framework to jointly control viewpoint, focal length, and parameterized photographic effects from a single image. Experiments show that ReX-Shot outperforms representative baselines across all three controls while enabling near-real-time interactive rephotography.

</details>


### [32] [Teach a Molmo2Fish: Towards interactive fish tracking with natural language guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18602)
*Kai Van Brunt,Justin Kay,Sara Beery*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Molmo2Fish，一种基于多模态大语言模型的交互式预测校正工作流程，用于声纳鱼类追踪数据集中的多目标追踪任务。研究发现该工具在鱼类追踪和轨迹校正任务上表现出高精度，但在自然语言引导方面仍有改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉常被用于生态学大数据的自动识别任务，但多目标追踪等复杂任务仍具挑战。研究者虽探索了人机协同方式来处理不完美的预测结果，但现有方法尚不充分，需要新的思路来提升生态学工作流中视觉模型的实用性。

Method: 提出一种交互式预测校正工作流程，以与多模态大语言模型对话的形式进行，并针对声纳鱼类追踪数据集进行定制化设计。在引导和非引导任务中测试该工具（Molmo2Fish），评估其校正自身预测轨迹和外部轨迹的能力。

Result: Molmo2Fish在鱼类追踪和轨迹校正任务上达到高性能，但将自然语言引导融入流程方面仍有很大改进空间。

Conclusion: 基于多模态大语言模型的对话式交互校正方法在生态学追踪任务中展现潜力，可作为概念验证，但自然语言引导能力有待进一步完善。

Abstract: Computer vision is increasingly used to automate recognition tasks in large ecological datasets, but more complex tasks such as multi-object tracking continue to pose challenges. As researchers seek to incorporate vision models in ecology workflows, various lines of research have explored how to make imperfect predictions useful through human-in-the-loop processes. We propose a new approach to working with imperfect tracking predictions through an interactive prediction correction workflow taking place as a conversation with a multimodal large language model, which we tailor to a sonar fish tracking dataset as an initial proof of concept. We investigate the performance of the tool, Molmo2Fish, across guided and unguided tasks, correcting its own predicted tracks and external tracks. We find that Molmo2Fish achieves high performance on fish tracking and track correction tasks, but there is still much room to improve on incorporating natural language guidance. The code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/tidalove/molmo2fish.

</details>


### [33] [VA-Judger: Reward Modeling from Human Preference Feedback for Joint Video-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18607)
*Yinming Huang,Shuyuan Tu,Xi Yan,Zihan Yang,Jianhua Han,Xu Hang,Yu-Gang Jiang,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VA-Judger，一个用于联合视频-音频生成的思维链全方位奖励模型。作者构建了大规模人类偏好数据集VAPref-10K（9K提示词、10.3K细粒度配对比较）和VA-Judger-Bench基准，通过三阶段训练（质量差距学习、拒绝采样蒸馏、维度级强化学习）来更准确地预测人类偏好，并将其用作奖励信号对生成模型进行后训练，显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过组合各质量维度（音频质量、视觉保真度、同步性）的独立指标来构建奖励信号，但这些指标分别评估感知维度，无法捕捉文本提示、视频和音频之间整体的语义和时间连贯性。优化这些指标易导致奖励黑客现象，生成的视频音频虽然在指标上得分高，但对人类观众而言却显得不连贯或不忠实。

Method: 1）构建VAPref-10K大规模人类偏好数据集和VA-Judger-Bench基准；2）提出VA-Judger链式思维全方位奖励模型，采用三阶段训练：先从质量差距明显的配对学习结构化输出和粗粒度偏好判别，再通过拒绝采样并为难度更高的近质量对比蒸馏可靠偏好解释（经人类标注验证），最后进行维度级强化学习，将人类反馈分解为各质量维度以获得比单一二值偏好标签更密集的奖励信号。

Result: VA-Judger在域内和域外评估中均优于基于指标的基线方法，在预测人类偏好方面表现更好。将其人类对齐的奖励用于音频-视频生成模型的后训练，显著改善了生成质量。

Conclusion: 基于人类偏好对齐的奖励模型比传统的多指标组合方法更能捕捉人类对视频音频内容整体连贯性的感知，将其用作强化学习奖励能够有效提升联合视频-音频生成模型的性能，并缓解奖励黑客问题。

Abstract: Using reinforcement learning to post-train joint video-audio generation models requires a reward signal. Existing methods construct this reward by combining metrics for individual quality dimensions, including audio quality, visual fidelity, and synchronization. However, these metrics evaluate perceptual dimensions separately and fail to capture the overall semantic and temporal coherence among the text prompt, video, and audio that shapes human preferences. Optimizing models against these metrics encourages reward hacking, generating video-audio content that achieves high scores on these metrics yet appears incoherent or unfaithful to human viewers. To address this problem, we first construct a large-scale human-preference dataset VAPref-10K for joint video-audio generation, comprising 9K prompts and 10.3K fine-grained paired comparisons from open-source generation models. We also introduce the VA-Judger-Bench benchmark with both in-domain and out-of-domain model comparisons to evaluate whether reward models truly align with human preferences. We further propose VA-Judger, a chain-of-thought omni-reward model for joint video-audio generation. In particular, VA-Judger first learns from pairs with clear quality gaps to establish structured output and coarse preference discrimination, then distills reliable preference explanations for harder near-quality comparisons via rejection sampling verified against human annotations, and finally performs dimension-wise reinforcement learning that decomposes human feedback into individual quality dimensions for denser reward signals than a single binary preference label. Experiments show that VA-Judger outperforms metric baselines in predicting human preferences on both in-domain and out-of-domain evaluations. Using its human-aligned rewards for post-training audio-video generation model also yields significant improvements in generation quality.

</details>


### [34] [CDGP: Contrastive Dual Gaussian Processes for Weakly Supervised Anomaly Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18614)
*Seungjun Chu,Seokhee Han,Mateusz Nowak,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CDGP是一个弱监督异常分割框架，利用对比双高斯过程同时建模正常与异常的预测分布，通过后验优势统计量结合联合预测不确定性来进行缺陷定位与图像级置信度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业视觉检测需要同时判断产品是否缺陷并定位缺陷位置，但像素级掩码标注成本高昂。现有方法仅从无缺陷图像学习并评分偏差，但真正的缺陷与异常但正常的区域都可能产生相似的偏差分数，难以区分。

Method: 提出对比双高斯过程（CDGP），对密集token建模正常和异常两类诱导变量的预测分布。使用后验优势统计量，将预测均值差除以联合预测不确定性进行标准化，提供空间证据和图像级置信度。证据与分层正常重建残差相结合以实现精细定位。所有校准仅使用训练数据。

Result: 在MVTec AD 2、KSDD2和VisA数据集上，CDGP在MVTec AD 2的所有定位指标上排名第一，在KSDD2和VisA上排名第一或具竞争力。消融研究验证了线性核GP公式的贡献与适用范围。

Conclusion: CDGP通过弱监督方式有效区分真正的缺陷与异常但正常的区域，无需像素标注即可实现优异的缺陷定位性能，其因子化和匹配的线性头控制实验为方法贡献与适用范围提供了清晰界定。

Abstract: Industrial visual inspection must both decide whether a product is defective and localize the defect, yet pixel-level masks are costly to collect at scale. Most anomaly-segmentation methods learn only from defect-free images and score deviations from normality. A true defect and an unusual-but-normal region, however, can both deviate substantially and receive similarly high scores. We propose Contrastive Dual Gaussian Processes (CDGP), a weakly supervised framework that models normal and anomaly inducing-variable predictive distributions over dense tokens. Its posterior-dominance statistic standardizes their predictive-mean difference by the joint predictive uncertainty, providing both spatial evidence and image-level confidence. This evidence complements hierarchical normal-reconstruction residuals for fine localization. All calibration uses training data only, without human pixel annotations or test-time fitting. Across MVTec AD~2, KSDD2, and VisA, CDGP ranks first among the evaluated methods on all MVTec AD~2 localization metrics and is first-place or competitive on KSDD2 and VisA. Factorized and matched linear-head controls delimit the contribution and scope of the linear-kernel Gaussian process (GP) formulation.

</details>
