Selective Autoregressive Supervision derives the exposed gradient from a masked surrogate loss rather than the full-label objective assumed by the attacker; Scale Blinding then applies randomized multiplicative rescaling, obscuring the gradient's natural magnitude; and Directional Privatization further randomizes the gradient direction while preserving its magnitude through the von Mises-Fisher (vMF) mechanism under a directional metric differential privacy guarantee. Crucially, utility is preserved: the Top segment still learns from all target tokens via Dual-Track Backpropagation, the exposed gradient remains informative since each supervised token retains its complete autoregressive context, and Bottom-Gradient Recovery restores the effective gradient for Bottom-segment optimization. Extensive experiments show that Gradient Mirage provides substantially stronger protection than existing defenses under comparable fine-tuning performance, achieving a better privacy-utility trade-off.

</details>


### [137] [Readable, Faithful, Used: Three Dissociable Properties of Demographic Identity in a Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18768)
*Fathin Difa Robbani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型中人口统计群体身份的内部表征，发现"可读、忠实排列、因果使用"是三种可分离的属性：注意力头在忠实度上优于标准残差读取（约达测量可靠性天花板的70%），但因果通路与忠实度不匹配，且即便最忠实的头部也难以恢复群体间意见排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常被用于模拟调查受访者，但其回答同质化且无法忠实反映真实的群体间差异。作者试图弄清：人口群体身份在LLM内部存储在哪里？其几何结构是否忠实反映真实群体意见结构？模型是否真正使用了这些编码？核心动机是厘清"LLM能否模拟人群"这一悬而未决的争论。

Method: 使用表征相似性分析（RSA）对照Pew地面真值数据（169个人口统计单元），在Mistral-7B中评估1,089个读取位置，并对六种属性类型进行因果干预。包括标准last-token残差读取、注意力头读取、因果干预实验、128维探针实验，以及在第二个模型家族的三个检查点上进行复现验证。

Result: (1)注意力头读取在六种类型中的五种优于标准残差读取，选择校正忠实度达rho=0.63；(2)单个头部（L11 H16）在全部六种类型中显著忠实，种族类型始终最弱且对提示敏感，跨模型家族复现；(3)因果使用不跟随忠实度，最清晰因果通路出现在最不忠实的类型中，替换整个身份对预测影响不足2%误差；(4)128维探针比模型自身答案更接近调查真值21-31%，但几乎无法恢复群体排序。

Conclusion: "可读的、忠实排列的、因果使用的"是同模型中三种可分离的属性。将这三者混为一谈正是"LLM能否模拟人群"争论悬而未决的根本原因。忠实编码不等于因果使用，也不等于能恢复群体间真实差异。

Abstract: Large language models are widely used to simulate survey respondents, yet their answers are homogeneous and unfaithful to real inter-group differences. We ask where demographic group identity lives inside an LLM, how faithfully its geometry mirrors real inter-group opinion structure, and whether it uses what it encodes. Using representational similarity analysis against Pew ground truth over 169 demographic cells, we score 1,089 read-out locations in Mistral-7B and intervene causally across six attribute types. Four results. (1) The standard last-token residual read-out understates the model: attention-head read-outs dominate it in five of six types, with selection-corrected fidelity up to rho=0.63 -- roughly 70% of the measurement-reliability ceiling -- surviving a lexical-similarity control. (2) A single head (L11 H16) is significantly faithful in all six types as a fixed location, while race-based types stay weak and prompt-fragile. Both phenomena replicate -- the analogous head significant in five of six types, weakest on the same race type -- across three checkpoints of a second model family, where ten billion training tokens barely move the map. (3) Causal use does not follow fidelity: the clearest causal pathway sits in one of the least faithful types (p=0.002, cluster-robust, fixed depth), the most faithful type shows no correction-surviving single-layer effect, and replacing the entire identity moves predictions by under 2% of their error. (4) A 128-dimensional probe of the single head lands 21-31% closer to survey truth than the model's own answers -- yet recovers almost none of the per-question group ordering, no better than the answers themselves. Readable, faithfully arranged, and causally used are three dissociable properties of the same model; treating them as one claim is what keeps the "can LLMs simulate populations" debate unresolved.

</details>


### [138] [Decomposing Wrong-Consensus Agreement in LLM Self-Consistency: A GPT-4.1 Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18795)
*Lizhuo Zhang,Mengmeng Tang,Chenfeng Long,Xiaoyong Tang,Xiang Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多元一致性指数Gamma，将多数投票的准确率增益分解为"机械性成分"（仅由逐案例的答案偏好决定）和"偏好无法解释的残差"，在GPT-4.1上对GPQA-Diamond和AIME基准进行量化分析，发现自一致性在难题上会回火，一致性只是分级证据而非认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票在多个LLM样本间被广泛用于提升准确率，但其增益变化不稳定，在难题上甚至可能适得其反。作者旨在对这一失败现象给出定量的解释，填补对投票增益机械性来源与真实信号之间区分的空白。

Method: 定义多元一致性指数Gamma为错误运行中与共识一致的样本期望比例，并按参考尺度d=(1-p)/(C-1)归一化。将Gamma分解为机械成分（仅由每案例答案偏好决定）与偏好无法解释的残差。机械零模型按难度匹配且无泄漏：每个案例以其自身准确率和选项偏好（由该案例的其他运行估计）重新模拟。采用GPT-4.1在两个基准上实验，并构建运行级偏好异质性参照。

Result: 在GPQA-Diamond上，逐案例答案偏好解释了81-93%的样本外测试运行一致性指数。在AIME上，机械偏好仅解释59-78%（若收缩为纯噪声则为21-29%），偏好无法解释的残差为1.56-2.80个Gamma单位。复现了硬问题的自一致性回火（分箱投票差距低至-0.09），最高一致性区间的准确率仅为0.42-0.83，比基准率提升1.2-3.6倍。

Conclusion: 共享偏差主导的叙事在GPQA-Diamond上过度宣称，因为错误但有吸引力的选项被逐案例偏好通道捕获；而AIME上存在实质性的偏好无法解释残差。一致性是分级证据而非认证，不提出新投票方法，代码与证据已提交可复现。

Abstract: Majority voting over multiple LLM samples is widely used to raise answer accuracy, yet its gain varies erratically: on hard questions it can even backfire. This paper gives a quantitative account of this failure. A pluralistic agreement index Gamma is defined as the expected fraction of the samples of a wrong run that agree with the consensus, normalized by a reference scale d=(1-p)/(C-1), and is decomposed into a mechanical component (what a vote delivers given only a per-case answer preference) and a preference-unexplained residual. The mechanical null is difficulty-matched and leak-free: each case is resimulated at its own accuracy and option preference, estimated from the case's other runs, so no run predicts its own agreement. On GPT-4.1 the decomposition shows benchmark-associated direction (an observational ordering over n=4 cells per benchmark, not a significance claim). On multiple-choice GPQA-Diamond, the per-case answer preference explains 81-93% of the held-out test-run agreement index: the shared-bias-dominates account over-claims here, because a wrong but attractive option the whole cohort latches onto is captured by the per-case preference channel (whether that preference is induced by shared training bias is not identified). On open-domain AIME, the mechanical preference explains only 59-78% (21-29% if shrunk to pure noise), and a preference-unexplained residual of 1.56-2.80 Gamma units survives, which a run-level preference-heterogeneity reference more than absorbs (1.4-2.1). A self-consistency backfire on hard questions is reproduced (binned voting gap down to -0.09, coupled CI [-0.12,-0.07]), and the highest-agreement bin reaches an accuracy of only 0.42-0.83, a 1.2-3.6x lift over base rate: agreement is graded evidence, not certification. No new voting method is proposed; code and evidence are committed and reproducible.

</details>


### [139] [Do Large Language Models Hallucinate Electric Fata Morganas?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18816)
*Kristina Šekrst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文认为AI幻觉不仅是一个工程缺陷，更在机器意识问题上具有哲学意义。作者通过两项实证研究（GPT模型在不同温度下的答题实验，以及编码器模型的事实性回答）证明：让模型显得有创造力和自发性的采样参数，恰好会增加其幻觉率；而幻觉源于训练数据的主客观多样性，而非认知能力的发展。作者据此论证，模型对情感或感知的自我报告属于幻觉范畴，未来即使出现机器意识，也可能因与高级幻觉无法区分而在认识论上不可及。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流观点将AI幻觉视为需要解决的工程缺陷，但作者认为幻觉现象与机器意识问题存在深层哲学关联。如果制造幻觉的参数与制造"创造性"表现的参数是同一批参数，那么基于行为测试的机器意识判断本身就值得怀疑——因为通过行为测试的模型可能只是在"幻觉"意识，这挑战了机器意识的可验证性基础。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与实证研究相结合的方法。理论部分梳理了幻觉的已知成因（源-目标发散、训练与推理的不一致、过拟合）。实证部分包含两个实验：(1) 使用连续几代GPT模型，在不同温度设置下回答模糊事实性问题，观察温度与回答准确性的关系；(2) 考察一个用百科全书数据训练的编码器模型，其对同类问题给出事实性且不加修饰的回答，以对照说明幻觉源于训练数据的社会多样性。

Result: 实验发现：高温设置下模型产生看似合理但错误的答案，低温下则给出事实准确的答案。编码器模型在百科全书数据上训练后能事实性回答问题而不产生幻觉，说明幻觉取决于训练数据的性质而非认知能力的发展。关键的采样参数正是使模型显得"创造性"和"自发性"并更可能通过行为智能测试的参数，同时也是提高幻觉率的参数。

Conclusion: 结合图灵、塞尔中文屋、框架问题和维纳/阿什比的控制论传统，作者总结：模型对情感或意识的自我报告符合幻觉的定义；未来若出现机器意识，它可能在认识论上不可及，因为任何意识的迹象都将与足够先进的幻觉无法区分。这既是对机器意识可检测性的根本质疑，也重新将幻觉从工程缺陷提升为理解机器思维本质的哲学入口。

Abstract: AI hallucinations - that is, outputs which are made up, cannot be verified, or contradict the source material - are generally regarded as an engineering flaw to be dealt with. This paper contends that they also have philosophical significance when it comes to the question of machine consciousness. We examine the known causes of hallucinations in large language models - such as source-target divergence, discrepancies between training and inference, and overfitting - and we present two empirical investigations. In the first, we apply successive generations of the GPT model to ambiguous factual questions under different temperature settings, finding that higher temperatures result in plausible but incorrect answers while lower temperatures lead to factually accurate ones. The sampling parameters that cause a model to seem creative or spontaneous and thus more likely to pass behavioral tests of intelligence are the same ones that increase its hallucination rate. In the second, we look at an encoder-only model that has been trained on encyclopedic data and which answers questions of the same type factually and without embellishment, indicating that hallucinations are due to exposure to subjective and socially diverse training data rather than to the development of any cognitive ability. Using references to Turing, Searle's Chinese Room, the frame problem, and the cybernetic tradition of Wiener and Ashby, we claim that a model's self-reports of emotion or sentience come within the definition of hallucination, and that any future occurrence of machine consciousness might remain epistemically inaccessible since it would be indistinguishable from a sufficiently advanced hallucination.

</details>


### [140] [Identifying Implicit Premises for Logical Reconstruction of Argument Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18821)
*Xuyao Feng,Anthony Hunter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经-符号混合流水线，利用大语言模型生成隐式前提，并将其转换为逻辑公式，与显式前提和主张的逻辑表示相结合，以展示语句之间的逻辑关系（蕴含、矛盾或中立），并在Microtext论辩语料库上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言文本重建论证图具有挑战性，主要因为省略三段论（含隐式前提的论证）普遍存在。现有方法中，NLP方法用于识别文本中的省略三段论，基于溯因的符号方法用于识别省略三段论的逻辑表示中缺失的前提，但缺乏生成隐式前提以逻辑上证明已知的蕴含或矛盾关系的方法。

Method: 提出一个神经-符号流水线：(1) 使用大语言模型(LLMs)生成中间隐式前提；(2) 将生成的隐式前提及显式前提、显式主张翻译为逻辑公式；(3) 使用这些逻辑公式展示语句间的逻辑关系（蕴含、矛盾或中立）。

Result: 该方法在Microtext论辩语料库上进行了评估。

Conclusion: 通过结合LLM的生成能力和符号逻辑的推理能力，该方法能够生成隐式前提以逻辑上证明语句间的蕴含或矛盾关系，弥补了现有方法在生成隐式前提方面的不足。

Abstract: The logical reconstruction of argument graphs from natural language text is challenging because of the prevalence of enthymemes (i.e., arguments with implicit premises). There are natural language processing methods for identifying enthymemes in text, and there are symbolic methods based on abduction for identifying missing premises in a logical representation of enthymemes. However, there is a need for methods to generate implicit premises to logically show a known entailment or contradiction relationship between a pair of statements. To address this, we propose a neuro-symbolic pipeline that uses large language models (LLMs) to generate intermediate implicit premises that are translated into logical formulae and used with logical formulae representing explicit premises and explicit claims to show the logical relationships between them (entailment, contradiction, or neutrality). Our approach is evaluated on the Microtext Argumentative Corpus.

</details>


### [141] [Understanding Multilingual Medical ASR Adaptation Through Layer-Wise Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18825)
*Souranil Kahali,Rituparna Bose,Abner Hernandez,Tomas Arias-Vergara,Andreas Maier,Ning Ma,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言医学微调如何重塑Whisper模型的内部编码器表示。通过逐层分析发现，英语医学微调主导编码器变化，多语言延续训练主要保留已适应的表示空间；领域和语言信息在各层高度可恢复，而可线性恢复的错误预测线索随着WER改善而减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学自动语音识别需要适应专业术语、有限的标注数据和多语言场景。虽然Whisper等预训练模型泛化能力强，但其在医学和多语言适配后的内部行为仅通过WER无法充分理解。

Method: 通过逐层编码器分析，比较了零样本解码、纯英文微调、德语诊断微调、两阶段EN->EN+DE延续训练，以及直接EN+DE微调等多种适配设置，并跨越不同大小的Whisper模型进行对比。

Result: Whisper-Medium在直接EN+DE训练下英语WER最低(7.72%)且综合WER最低(26.30%)；德语专用Whisper-Large-v3德语WER最低(44.96%)但属于语料内诊断而非稳健泛化。两阶段训练的逐层分析显示英语医学微调主导编码器变化，多语言延续保留已适应表示空间。

Conclusion: 最佳模型取决于适配设置；领域和语言信息在各层高度可恢复，而错误预测线索随WER改善而线性减弱，揭示了多语言医学适配中表示空间的动态变化规律。

Abstract: Medical automatic speech recognition (MedASR) requires adaptation to specialised terminology, limited annotated clinical data, and multilingual use cases. Although large-scale pretrained ASR models such as Whisper achieve strong generalisation, their behaviour after medical and multilingual adaptation remains insufficiently understood beyond word error rate (WER). This paper investigates how multilingual medical adaptation reshapes the internal representations of Whisper models through layer-wise encoder analysis. We compare zero-shot decoding, English-only fine-tuning, German-only diagnostic fine-tuning, two-stage EN->EN+DE continuation, and direct EN+DE fine-tuning across Whisper model sizes. Fine-tuning substantially improves MedASR performance, but the best model depends on the adaptation setting: Whisper-Medium gives the lowest English WER (7.72%) and the lowest combined EN+DE WER under direct EN+DE training (26.30%); German-only Whisper-Large-v3 gives the lowest German WER (44.96%), but as a within-corpus diagnostic on 86 single-speaker training utterances rather than robust generalisation. Layer-wise analysis of the two-stage Whisper-Small trajectory shows that English medical fine-tuning produces the dominant encoder shift, whereas multilingual continuation largely preserves the adapted representation space. Domain and language information remain highly recoverable across layers, while linearly recoverable error-predictive cues weaken as WER improves.

</details>


### [142] [Assessing Quality of Experience in Natural Language Generation of German Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18888)
*Dinh Nam Pham,Shushen Manakhimova,Vivien Macketanz,Sebastian Möller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TextQ-German，一个用于德语自然语言生成（NLG）从体验质量（QoE）角度进行人工中心评估的数据集套件，涵盖自动文本摘要和机器翻译两个任务。通过众包研究收集德语母语者的质量评分，开发了基于Transformer、语言学特征及混合方法的自动QoE预测模型。混合模型在几乎所有实验设置中优于纯Transformer基线，语言学特征单独使用也能接近微调语言模型的性能。数据集还扩展了LLM生成的输出并标注了总体QoE评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动评估指标无法捕捉NLG生成文本质量的多维度特性，而LLM等系统已广泛应用于实际场景，需要更可靠、以人类为中心的质量评估方法。作者希望从QoE视角为德语NLG建立人工评估资源，弥补现有评估体系的不足。

Method: 构建TextQ-German数据集套件：通过以德语母语者为对象的众包研究，收集自动文本摘要和机器翻译任务的人工质量评分，识别各任务相关的感知质量维度。开发三类自动QoE预测模型：基于Transformer的模型、基于语言学特征的模型以及混合模型，并扩展数据集加入LLM生成输出的总体QoE评分。

Result: 混合模型在几乎所有实验设置中优于纯Transformer基线；语言学特征单独使用即可接近微调语言模型的性能；在保留集上的最终验证表明模型能够泛化到未见数据。

Conclusion: 该工作为NLG评估提供了公开可用的资源和自动QoE预测基线，为开发更符合人类质量感知的NLG系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Natural Language Generation (NLG) has made the reliable evaluation of generated text increasingly critical, as these systems, such as large language models (LLMs), are now widely deployed in real-world applications. However, traditional automatic metrics fail to capture the multifaceted nature of perceived quality. In this paper, we introduce TextQ-German, a novel dataset suite for human-centered evaluation of German NLG from a Quality of Experience (QoE) perspective, covering automatic text summarization and machine translation. Through crowdsourcing studies with German speakers, we collect human quality ratings and identify relevant perceptual quality dimensions for each task. We develop automatic QoE prediction models, including transformer-based, linguistic feature-based, and hybrid approaches. Hybrid models outperform pure transformer baselines in almost all experimental settings, while linguistic features alone can approach the performance of fine-tuned language models. The dataset is extended with LLM-generated outputs annotated with overall QoE scores. Final validation on held-out sets indicates generalization to unseen data. Our work contributes a publicly accessible resource for NLG evaluation and baselines for automatic QoE prediction, providing a foundation for developing NLG systems that better align with human quality perception.

</details>


### [143] [SMTrap: Cost-Effective DoS Attacks Against Large Reasoning Models via SMT Conflict Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18921)
*Jian Yang,Zhenqi Feng,Zhaoyang Yu,Zhaoxin Fan,Kejian Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Jianjun Huang,Wei You,Bin Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SMTrap，一种无需模型反馈的LRM-DoS攻击框架。它利用SMT求解器的冲突计数作为外部信号，引导生成推理密集的CSP实例，使LRM陷入深度回溯搜索并产生冗长输出轨迹，从而以极低成本（仅CPU）实现比现有方法强数倍的拒绝服务攻击效果，并提出了相应的工具化缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LRM-DoS方法依赖模型反馈（重复查询目标模型或训练专用攻击模型），成本高昂，严重削弱攻击杠杆。因此需要一种无需模型反馈的高效低成本攻击范式。

Method: 提出search amplification范式：利用SMT求解器产生的冲突计数作为低成本外部信号，指导生成推理密集的CSP实例。核心观察是LRM解CSP时依赖试错回溯搜索，SMT冲突计数越高，LRM回溯搜索越深入、输出轨迹越长。基于此构建SMTrap框架，仅需CPU即可运行，无需模型查询、攻击模型训练或GPU。

Result: 在七个前沿模型上的评估显示，SMTrap达到最先进的LRM-DoS效果，产生的拒绝服务效果比现有基线强数倍。同时验证了工具化缓解方案能显著降低token消耗。

Conclusion: SMTrap证明了利用求解器外部信号（如SMT冲突计数）可以实现高效、低成本的LRM-DoS攻击，为模型安全问题提供了新视角，同时也展示了可行的缓解方向。

Abstract: Existing LRM-DoS methods rely heavily on model feedback to synthesize attack queries, requiring either repeated queries to the target model or training a dedicated attack model. These expensive operations severely weaken attack leverage. In this paper, we propose \emph{search amplification}, a novel, model-feedback-free LRM-DoS paradigm. It employs the conflict count derived from an Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) solver as a low-cost external signal to guide the synthesis of inference-heavy Constraint Satisfaction Problem (CSP) instances. Our key observation is that LRMs depend on trial-and-backtracking search when solving CSPs, where higher SMT conflict counts on a given CSP instance positively correlate with more extensive LRM backtracking search and substantially longer output trajectories. Building on this finding, we propose \textsc{SMTrap}, a lightweight, CPU-only framework. Guided by SMT conflict counts, \textsc{SMTrap} generates inference-heavy CSP queries without model queries, attack-model training, or GPU computation. Evaluations across seven frontier models demonstrate the state-of-the-art LRM-DoS capability of \textsc{SMTrap}, producing DoS effects multiple times stronger than existing baselines. To mitigate the threat of \textsc{SMTrap}, we demonstrate a tool-based mitigation that significantly cuts token usage.

</details>


### [144] [Test-Time Scaling in the Wild: Why Exploitation, Not Exploration, Is the Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18931)
*Davide Romano,Kanak Raj,Jerrod Parker,Daniele Giofrè*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对五种测试时扩展（TTS）技术家族在五个开放式生成基准（医学、法律、金融、通用聊天、创意写作）上进行计算归一化比较，通过统一的"探索-利用"框架分解各方法的token预算效果。研究发现：扩展探索（增加候选池）有效，最佳候选质量随计算量稳定提升；但利用环节（从候选池中选出最终输出）失效——奖励模型与真实质量相关性仅约ρ≈0.12，导致选择接近随机；树搜索因多样性崩溃放大此问题；修正仅在一个基准上有效；只有融合（Fusion）方法持续优于单样本基线，但仍只能恢复约40%可用质量。结论：瓶颈不在候选池，而在于从中选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTS技术（多数采样、搜索、迭代修正）在数学和代码任务上表现优异，但这类任务的验证较为直接。作者指出这些技术几乎只在验证简单的任务上被开发和压力测试，尚未在开放式生成任务（如医学、法律、金融、创意写作）中进行系统的计算归一化比较，因此缺乏对这些方法在验证复杂场景下的真实有效性的理解。

Method: 建立了统一的"探索-利用"分解框架来分析每种TTS方法token预算的有效性。在五个开放式生成基准上，对五种TTS家族（采样多候选、树搜索、迭代修正、融合、以及其他搜索策略）进行了计算归一化对比实验，并使用最新（state-of-the-art）生成器和奖励模型进行评估。

Result: 探索扩展有效：最佳候选质量在全部场景中随计算量稳定提升。但利用环节失效：奖励模型与真实质量相关性仅为ρ≈0.12，使选择接近随机。树搜索通过多样性崩溃放大此失败；迭代修正仅在五个基准中的一个有效，其他场景的表观收益存在混淆因素；融合（Fusion）是唯一在全部基准上一致优于单样本基线的方法，但仍只能恢复约40%的可用质量。

Conclusion: TTS在开放式生成任务中的核心瓶颈不是候选池的生成（探索），而是从池中选取最终输出的过程（利用）。现有奖励模型无法可靠区分候选质量，这一发现表明需要重新思考开放式生成任务中TTS方法的设计重点——从扩展探索转向改进选择/利用机制。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves language model outputs by spending additional inference compute - generating multiple candidates, searching over partial sequences, or iteratively refining drafts. These techniques yield large gains on mathematics and code, but have been developed and stress-tested almost exclusively on tasks where verification is straightforward. We conduct the first compute-normalised comparison of five TTS families across five open-ended generation benchmarks spanning medicine, law, finance, general chat, and creative writing - grounded in a unified framework that decomposes the effectiveness of each method's token budget into exploration and exploitation. The answer depends on which side of that decomposition you examine. Scaling exploration works: the best candidate in the pool improves steadily with compute across all settings. What breaks is exploitation - the step that converts a rich candidate pool into a final output. With state-of-the-art generators, reward models correlate at only $ρ_v \approx 0.12$ with true quality, rendering selection near-random regardless of budget. Tree search amplifies this failure through diversity collapse. Refinement helps on one of five benchmarks; its apparent gains elsewhere are confounded. Only synthesis across candidates (Fusion) consistently improves over single-sample baselines, yet still recovers only ~40% of available quality. The candidate pool is not the bottleneck - choosing from it is.

</details>


### [145] [MedUAG: Unified Understanding and Generation for Medical Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18937)
*Zijie Meng,Yuncheng Zhang,Hualiang Wang,Yitian Tang,Xiaotang Gai,Chen Shen,Songtao Jiang,Shaosheng Cao,Jian Wu,Xian Wu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了医疗多模态统一理解与生成（UAG）的基础框架，包含三个核心贡献：构建了迄今最大规模的医疗UAG数据集MedUAGCorpus（超过600万实例，覆盖14种成像模态）、设计了涵盖12个任务的系统化评测基准MedUAGBench，以及开发了端到端训练的统一医疗模型MedUAG，并在广泛的理解与生成任务上取得了强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）正快速演变为统一理解与生成（UAG）框架，但在医疗领域的扩展面临两大障碍：缺乏全面的训练和评测基准，以及缺少经过广泛验证的统一医疗模型。

Method: 1) 构建MedUAGCorpus——目前最大的医疗统一理解与生成数据集，包含超过600万实例、覆盖14种成像模态；2) 提出MedUAGBench——一个系统化基准，将医疗生成评测扩展到12个不同任务，采用标准化协议；3) 开发MedUAG——一个端到端训练的统一医疗模型，利用上述数据和基准进行训练与验证。

Result: 广泛实验表明，MedUAG在多种理解和生成任务上取得了强劲性能，建立了有竞争力的基线。

Conclusion: 该工作为下一代医疗多模态系统铺平了道路，填补了医疗领域统一理解与生成框架的空白。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are rapidly evolving into unified understanding and generation (UAG) frameworks. However, extending these unified paradigms to the medical domain is hindered by: the absence of comprehensive training and evaluation benchmarks, and the lack of broadly validated unified medical model. To address these gaps, we present a comprehensive foundation for medical UAG. First, we construct MedUAGCorpus, the largest unified medical understanding and generation dataset to date, comprising over 6 million instances across 14 imaging modalities. Second, we introduce MedUAGBench, a systematic benchmark that expands medical generation evaluation to 12 diverse tasks under standardized protocols. Finally, leveraging these resources, we develop MedUAG, an end-to-end trained unified medical model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MedUAG achieves strong performance across a wide array of understanding and generation tasks, establishing a competitive baseline and paving the way for next-generation medical multimodal systems.

</details>


### [146] [Institutional Newspapers Pipeline: Deriving billions of high quality tokens from historical newspapers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18972)
*Matteo Cargnelutti,Catherine Brobston,Eben English,Jake Sadow,Kacie Bailey,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jessica Chapel,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了与波士顿公共图书馆联合设计的机构报纸流水线（Institutional Newspapers Pipeline），一个模块化系统，用于从历史报纸扫描件中提取高质量结构化数据，并在工作站级硬件上运行，最终发布了包含16.3亿token、8310万个人物片段、147万+张报纸扫描件的开放数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史报纸是公共生活的丰富记录，但其版面密集、不规则且含噪，使得计算化访问既具挑战又受限。需要一种能高效提取高质量结构化数据的方法。

Method: 采用模块化的多步骤流水线架构：首先将扫描件分割为类型无关的裁剪片段，对每个片段进行OCR，随后执行文本分析、类型分类、阅读顺序检测、命名实体识别、主题分类、语言检测以及预计算嵌入生成。整个流水线设计为可解释、可定制，且计算开销低至可在工作站级硬件运行。

Result: 对波士顿公共图书馆的部分馆藏运行该流水线，OCR输出涵盖16.3亿个o200k_base token、8310万个独立裁剪片段，来源于1795年至1930年间出版的1,473,635张公有领域报纸扫描件。论文报告了各处理步骤的方法、训练的小模型、评估结果及数据规模测量。

Conclusion: 该工作被认为是向解锁数千万张报纸扫描件中高质量数据迈出的重要一步，同时提供了流水线、模型和数据集的开放发布。

Abstract: Historical newspapers are an abundant record of public life, but their dense, irregular and sometimes noisy layouts make computational access to these materials both challenging and limited. We present the Institutional Newspapers Pipeline, a modular system we jointly designed with Boston Public Library to extract high-quality, structured datasets from historical newspaper scans. It was architected so that each step remains interpretable and customizable, and so that the pipeline as a whole remains computationally frugal enough to run on workstation-level hardware. The pipeline runs each scan through a multi-step process: it segments scans into individual type-agnostic crops and performs OCR on each resulting segment before then performing text analysis, type classification, reading order detection, named entities recognition, subject classification, language detection, and pre-computed embeddings generation on every crop. We ran this pipeline against a portion of Boston Public Library's holdings and released the results as an open dataset. The optical character recognition (OCR) output represents 16.3 billion o200k_base tokens across 83.1 million individual crops, extracted from 1,473,635 public domain newspaper scans published between 1795 and 1930. This report describes our methods for each processing step, the small models we trained, as well as the evaluation results and dataset-scale measurements we collected in the process. It accompanies the release of the pipeline, models, and dataset. We position this work as a substantial step towards unlocking high-quality data from tens of millions of newspaper scans.

</details>


### [147] [DeepWeaver: Bridging the Evidence Synthesis Gap in Open-Ended Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18988)
*Xujia Wang,Yizhe Zhang,Bin Xu,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeepWeaver 是一个通过维护"思维块链"（TBCs）来弥合检索与生成之间"证据综合鸿沟"的框架。它使用结构化表示来组织证据，并通过多轮审视残余证据、修订TBC和发现新主张来提升开放域问答的答案质量，同时引入高密度基准LoQA进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）的"检索-生成"管线在处理开放域深度研究问题时，单纯的检索不足以生成高质量答案。直接生成存在三个问题：证据利用不足、引用错位、将多样化信息压缩为浅层摘要，即所谓的"证据综合鸿沟"（evidence synthesis gap）——检索与生成之间的鸿沟。

Method: DeepWeaver 提出"思维块链"（Thought Block Chains, TBCs）作为结构化表示，将主张（claims）、重要信息、关键词和支持证据分组组织。框架通过从属TBCs审视残余证据、提交TBC修订、在最终生成前发现新主张，实现证据的迭代编织（evidence weaving）。

Result: 在知识库和网络两种开放域问答场景下评估，并引入高密度综合基准LoQA。在多种LLM上，DeepWeaver在LoQA上提升了内容充分性、引用依据性和细节保留；在DeepResearch Bench上获得了更深层次的洞察和更高的引用质量。

Conclusion: 证据编织（evidence weaving）是弥合开放域问答中检索与生成之间鸿沟的有效机制，为检索增强生成提供了一种新的结构化中间表示方法。

Abstract: Retrieve-then-generate pipelines are commonly used to produce deep-research answers for open-ended questions, but retrieval alone is insufficient: LLMs must organize noisy and fragmented evidence into comprehensive, well-cited answers. We refer to this process as evidence synthesis. However, direct generation often underuses evidence, misaligns citations, and collapses diverse information into shallow summaries, exposing an evidence synthesis gap between retrieval and generation. Thus, we propose DeepWeaver, a novel framework that weaves noisy retrieved evidence into comprehensive answers by maintaining Thought Block Chains (TBCs), a structured representation that groups claims, salient information, keywords, and supporting evidence. DeepWeaver uses subordinate TBCs to inspect residual evidence, commit TBC revisions, and discover new claims before final generation. We evaluate DeepWeaver on open-ended QA over both knowledge bases and the web, and introduce LoQA, a high-density benchmark for evidence synthesis. Across multiple LLMs, DeepWeaver improves content sufficiency, citation grounding, and detail preservation on LoQA, while achieving deeper insights and higher citation quality on DeepResearch Bench. These results show that evidence weaving is an effective mechanism for bridging retrieval and generation in open-ended QA. Our code is available at https://github.com/KlozeWang/DeepWeaver.

</details>


### [148] [Structure, Association, and Decision Value: Representation-Based Difficulty Estimation for Adaptive Inference in African-Language NLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19003)
*Toheeb Ogunade*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言非洲NLP中内部表示统计量是否能为自适应推理提供有效的样例难度信号，发现不能。通过15种非洲语言的NLI实验，作者报告了四个发现：AfriXNLI的英语配置与XNLI评估数据有1047/1050个样本逐字共享，存在测试暴露问题；参数量不能可靠排序非洲语言能力；角分散度比有效秩更具语言决定性；能抵抗语言控制的信号与目标相关且两者仅0.655相关。最终结论是，在测试的条件下没有信号能使自适应路由优于始终高成本推理，但oracle在60%计算量下仍超出11个准确率点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以利用内部表示统计量作为样例级难度信号，在非洲多语言NLP中实现自适应推理（自适应地将困难样本路由到昂贵模型，简单样本路由到便宜模型），以实现计算效率与准确率的平衡。

Method: 在15种非洲语言上使用冻结的现成checkpoint进行自然语言推断（NLI）研究。评估多种表示统计量（角分散度、有效秩等）作为难度信号的预测能力，并进行语言控制（language-controlled）的关联分析。比较了不同规模checkpoint的能力差异，并测试自适应路由策略相对于始终使用昂贵推理的收益。

Result: 四个主要发现：1) AfriXNLI的英语/法语/斯瓦希里语配置派生自XNLI，存在测试数据暴露问题（1047/1050样本重复，某checkpoint在该split上得1.000分）；2) 参数量不保证能力排序（更大模型在7种语言更好，8种更差）；3) 角分散度比有效秩更受语言决定，池化相关会夸大一个掩盖另一个；4) 幸存语言控制的信号与目标依赖：有效秩预测增益概率但不预测预测改变，廉价模型置信度则相反，两目标仅0.655相关。没有信号使自适应路由优于始终昂贵推理，oracle在60%计算量下仍超11个准确率点。

Conclusion: 中心方法论发现是：一个表示统计量可以在某一计算收益概念上统计显著，却对另一概念无关，因此可能是糟糕的决策变量。在测试的模型、信号和计算预算下，自适应推理目前不可行，尽管oracle表明理论上存在潜力。

Abstract: We ask whether internal representation statistics can provide useful example-level difficulty signals for adaptive inference in multilingual African NLP, and find that they cannot in this setting. Studying natural language inference across 15 African languages with frozen off-the-shelf checkpoints, we report four results. First, AfriXNLI's English configuration shares 1,047 of its 1,050 examples verbatim with XNLI evaluation data, and one widely used NLI checkpoint scores 1.000 on that test split, consistent with XNLI test exposure. Because AfriXNLI is derived from XNLI, its English, French and Swahili configurations cannot serve as clean evaluations for XNLI-trained models. Second, parameter count does not reliably order capability across African languages: our larger checkpoint is better in seven languages and worse in eight, with no significant aggregate difference. Third, across three multilingual representation spaces, angular dispersion is consistently more language-determined than effective rank, so pooled correlations can inflate one and mask the other. Fourth, the association that survives language control depends on the target: effective rank predicts probability gain from escalation but not whether escalation changes the prediction, while cheap-model confidence shows the opposite pattern; the two targets correlate at only 0.655. Under the tested models, signals, and compute budgets, no evaluated signal makes adaptive routing preferable to always-expensive inference, although an oracle exceeds it by 11 accuracy points at 60% of the compute. Our central methodological finding is that a representation statistic can be statistically significant for one notion of computational benefit while being irrelevant to another, and therefore be a poor decision variable.

</details>


### [149] [Introducing the Privacy-HSD Trade-off: Hate Speech Detection, but not at the Cost of Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19006)
*Stephen Meisenbacher,Vlad Garbuz,Chirill Donos,Maxim Dnestreanschii,Gabriel Creanga,Andreea-Elena Bodea,Thomas Lampert,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了仇恨言论检测（HSD）系统与用户隐私之间的冲突，发现HSD系统可能在追求性能的同时无意间编码作者身份信息，构成隐私威胁。作者提出了隐私-HSD权衡的概念，并基准测试了多种文本私有化方法及新提出的AgnoSpeech技术，证明平衡隐私与HSD性能困难但可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论是影响大量在线用户（尤其是青年和少数群体）的现实威胁，构建可靠的自动HSD系统至关重要，但同时必须平衡个人隐私权。现有研究很少探讨HSD系统在性能与隐私之间的潜在冲突，作者希望揭示并解决这一矛盾。

Method: 1) 通过实证研究展示HSD系统可能以编码作者身份为代价来获得性能，构成隐私威胁；2) 提出"隐私-HSD权衡"的概念框架；3) 基准测试一系列文本私有化方法；4) 提出新的领域特定方法AgnoSpeech。

Result: 证明HSD系统确实存在隐私泄露风险；发现平衡隐私与HSD性能是困难的，但通过适当的私有化方法（包括AgnoSpeech）是可以实现的。

Conclusion: 隐私与HSD之间存在需要谨慎权衡的关系，这为未来在保护隐私前提下维护在线参与安全的研究指明了方向，呼吁更多研究关注这一交叉领域。

Abstract: Hate speech is a real and timely threat that affects a large portion of online users, especially youth and minority groups. While building reliable and robust automatic hate speech detection (HSD) systems is paramount, we argue that this must also be balanced with the individual right to privacy. Exploring the intersection of HSD and privacy, we demonstrate that HSD systems might unintentionally achieve performance at the cost of encoding authorship, posing a threat to privacy. Building on these findings, we establish the notion of a privacy-HSD trade-off, which demands a careful balance. We benchmark a series of text privatization methods, as well as our newly proposed domain-specific AgnoSpeech technique, showing that balancing privacy and HSD is difficult but feasible. The findings make a strong case for more research on the trade-offs between privacy and HSD, both of which have tangible implications for the safeguarding of online participation.

</details>


### [150] [Grading the Graders: Verification Autonomy Levels (L0-L5) for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19009)
*Yajie Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出验证自主级别（VAL）元标准，将大语言模型验证方案沿单一轴分类：验证规范来源及结论所保证的内容。VAL范围从L0（LLM自声明）到L3/L4（可判定系统的单性质或域级完备性），L5在无约束情况下不可达。识别了完备性盲点，并指出完备性仅对形式化可规范属性可达，经验性开放世界验证只能达到锚定正确性（L2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证文献中"level"一词被混淆使用为至少五种不同含义（验证粒度、概念抽象、风险层级、系统栈层、真值认识论来源），导致系统性概念混淆。现有文献未明确区分完备性与锚定正确性之间的本质差异，需要统一的元标准来澄清这些概念。

Method: 提出VAL（Verification Autonomy Levels）分类学，沿单一轴（验证规范来源及结论保证）将验证方案分为L0-L5级别。识别完备性盲点：基于替换和采样的验证器可确认候选成立，但无法证明没有候选被遗漏。通过四个领域（符号数学、行为监控、医学诊断、代码生成）及最强形式化验证基线进行验证，并论证粒度、概念层次、风险与系统栈与VAL正交。

Result: 确立了验证完备性与锚定正确性的二分：完备性仅对形式化可规范属性可达，而实证开放世界验证（事实核查、诊断）上限为L2。通过17篇论文展示了系统性的"level"概念混用，并证明现有验证文献中的各维度与VAL正交。

Conclusion: VAL为LLM验证领域提供了清晰的概念坐标系，能解决文献中的术语混乱。核心发现是完备性验证与开放世界验证之间存在根本性能力边界，这为未来验证方法的设计和比较提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly paired with verifiers (step checkers, self-consistency filters, tool-based fact checkers, formal proof assistants) that claim to detect the model's errors. Yet the verification literature uses the word "level" to mean at least five different things: verification granularity, concept abstraction, risk tier, system-stack layer, and the epistemic source of the ground truth. We propose Verification Autonomy Levels (VAL), a meta-standard classifying verification schemes along a single axis: where does the verification spec come from, and what does the verdict guarantee? VAL ranges from L0 (LLM self-declaration, no deterministic anchor) through L2 (objective ground truth, correctness only) to L3/L4 (decidable systems with single-property or domain-level completeness), with L5 impossible in the unrestricted case. Central to VAL is the completeness blind spot: substitution- and sampling-based verifiers can confirm that proposed candidates hold, but cannot prove that no candidate was missed. We further identify a dichotomy the literature has not stated: completeness is reachable only for formally specifiable properties, while empirical open-world verification (fact-checking, diagnosis) caps at anchored correctness (L2). We document this across four domains (symbolic mathematics, behavior monitoring, medical diagnosis, and code generation) and in the strongest existing formal-verification baseline, whose authors note the verifier "focuses on the correctness of each step." We show the levels of granularity, concept hierarchy, risk, and system stack are orthogonal to VAL, resolving a systematic conflation across 17 surveyed papers. Code and full assessment are released as supplementary material.

</details>


### [151] [Institutional Books - Enriched Text: A customizable multilingual open-source pipeline for denoising, deduplicating, and annotating OCR text at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19026)
*David Lowry-Duda,Matteo Cargnelutti,Catherine Brobston,Salwa Ismail,Greg Leppert,Amanda Watson,Jonathan Zittrain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IB-HL (Institutional Books: Harvard Library)，一个包含983,004卷文献的语料库，并提出了一种称为"Enriched Text"的新型数据预处理方法。该方法不生成单一的"完整"token流，而是通过HTML样式的注解在文本上保留元数据、段落语言检测、重复段落聚类和每段落bits-per-byte评分，让用户根据自身需求定制输出，而非接受全局编辑决策。发布内容包括IB-HL-ET（包含217B tokens的增强文本版本，组织成13.9亿个带注解的子主题段落）及其生成流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IB-HL数据集发布后，研究者在使用时发现标准的大规模预处理实践（如激进过滤、去重、语言限制、丢弃元数据）与信息审慎管理目标之间存在张力。现有流水线主要为网页文本优化，不适合图书这类需要保留语义和元数据的文本；同时不同研究者重复进行相似的处理和分析工作，造成资源浪费。

Method: 提出"Enriched Text"方法：(1) 在归一化文本的同时通过注解保留元数据；(2) 分离endmatter（书末附属内容）；(3) 逐段落检测语言；(4) 识别重复段落簇；(5) 计算逐段落bits-per-byte评分。所有信息以HTML类注解分层叠加在文本上，用户可通过解析注解按需定制输出，流水线适用于约250种语言。

Result: 发布包含IB-HL-ET增强文本版本：983,003卷、217B o200k_base tokens、组织成13.9亿带注解的子主题段落，以及生成该版本的完整流水线。

Conclusion: Enriched Text方法通过注解而非过滤的方式处理大规模图书语料，使得语料库对人类研究者和机器解析都更加友好，避免了全局编辑决策对特定研究需求的限制，为大规模文本数据的负责任管理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Released in 2025, Institutional Books: Harvard Library (IB-HL) is a collection of 983,004 volumes (242B o200k_base tokens), originally digitized through Harvard Library's participation in the Google Books Library project. As researchers and developers have begun to use IB-HL, a tension has emerged between standard large-scale preprocessing practices and the goals of careful information stewardship. Many existing pipelines optimize for web text: as a result, they tend to aggressively filter, deduplicate, restrict by language, and sometimes discard meaningful metadata. Meanwhile, researchers seeking to use IB-HL duplicate effort while performing similar processing and analysis.
  We describe an approach that we call Enriched Text. Instead of producing a single 'complete' stream of tokens, we normalize the text while preserving metadata through annotations. We separate endmatter, detect per-paragraph language, identify clusters of duplicate paragraphs, and compute per-paragraph bits-per-byte scores. We provide this information through HTML-like annotations layered on top of the text. By parsing these annotations, users can tailor the output to their own needs instead of accepting a global editorial decision on content. The pipeline applies to all $\approx$250 languages in the collection.
  This report describes this project's goals, implementation, and design rationale. The release includes IB-HL-ET (an enriched-text version of IB-HL containing 217B o200k_base tokens across 983,003 volumes, organized into 1.39B annotated subtopic paragraphs) and the pipeline that produced it. These serve to make the collection easier for machines to parse and for humans to study.

</details>


### [152] [Intercepting the Kangaroo: Experimental Astrolinguistics with Constructed Lexicons, Active Probing, and Large Language Models as Informants and Hypothesis Proposers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19124)
*Francesco Cordella,Mauro Cappelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将"天体语言学"（Astrolinguistics）从纯思辨变为实验科学。作者构建了两个词汇系统刻意不兼容的语言模型（一个编码形状/颜色/运动，另一个将颜色与运动融合并缺乏形状），通过一个完全脚本化的编排器在两种范畴系统间进行翻译。核心失败模式是"袋鼠效应"——词语被静默附加到错误所指（即奎因的翻译不确定性操作化）。实验表明，结合跨情境消除、预注册预测探针、主动场景选择、严格恢复轮和隔离区的协议在400多次运行中零未检测误译，且覆盖率超过被动基线。面对注入的袋鼠陷阱，朴素直示法和纯统计学习100%失败，完整协议则全部拦截。在信息提供者噪声下优雅降级，对假设空间外的词通过LLM提议规则+脚本验证的生成-测试循环实现恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天体语言学（与范畴化现实方式不同的心智通信）自Freudenthal的Lincos（1960）以来一直纯粹是推测性的，没有实验基础。作者希望将其变为可实验的科学，通过操作化奎因的翻译不确定性理论，用语言模型实现可控的跨范畴系统翻译实验。

Method: 构建两个词汇系统刻意不相容的语言模型作为信息提供者（完全掌握真值），用一个完全脚本化的编排器在两种范畴系统间翻译。协议包括：跨情境消除、预注册预测探针、主动场景选择、严格恢复轮、隔离区。对假设空间外的词使用生成-测试循环：LLM提出规则，脚本验证。实验覆盖400+次模拟和实况运行，并注入"袋鼠陷阱"测试鲁棒性。

Result: 完整协议在测试条件下零未检测误译，覆盖率超过被动基线（d=0.62）。注入袋鼠陷阱后：朴素直示法和纯统计学习100%失败，完整协议拦截所有诱饵并在缺乏判别证据时声明奎因等价类而非猜测。在信息噪声下：2%词级噪声时零袋鼠持续存在；10%噪声时主要弃权而非出错。对假设空间外的词，覆盖率随提议者能力扩展（0%→18%→72%→100%），未检测误译始终为零。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下，正确性是协议的性质，覆盖率是工具的性质。即协议的可靠性由方法设计保证（可独立于具体工具），而覆盖广度取决于所用工具（如LLM）的能力。这为天体语言学开创了实验范式，并展示了形式化协议如何驯服语言翻译中的根本不确定性。

Abstract: Astrolinguistics -- communication with minds that categorize reality differently from ours -- has been purely speculative since Freudenthal's Lincos (1960). We make it experimental. Two language models with deliberately incompatible constructed lexicons (one encoding shape, color, and motion; the other fusing color with motion, encoding parity, and lacking shape) serve as informants with complete ground truth, while a fully scripted orchestrator translates between the two category systems. The central failure mode is the kangaroo effect: the silent attachment of a word to the wrong referent -- Quine's indeterminacy of translation, operationalized. Across 400+ simulated and live runs, a protocol combining cross-situational elimination, pre-registered predictive probes, active scene selection, a stricter recovery round, and quarantine produced no undetected mistranslations under the tested conditions and exceeded a passive baseline's coverage (d = 0.62). Injected kangaroo traps defeated naive ostension and pure statistical learning in 100% of runs, while the full protocol intercepted every decoy and, where discriminating evidence is ontologically unavailable, declared Quinean equivalence classes instead of guessing. Under informant noise it degrades gracefully: zero kangaroos persist up to 2% per-word noise; at 10% the protocol predominantly abstains rather than errs. Finally, words outside the scripted hypothesis space (a history-dependent relational term and an XOR contextual homonym) are recovered by a generate-and-test loop in which an LLM proposes rules and the script verifies them: coverage scales with proposer capability (0% -> 18% -> 72% -> 100%) while undetected mistranslations stayed at zero throughout. In the tested conditions, correctness is a property of the protocol; coverage is a property of the instruments.

</details>


### [153] [Comment-level Topic Drift Analysis in the Reddit Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19133)
*Steven Morse,Daniel Runfola,Trenton W. Ford*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于嵌入的动态主题建模方法，用于在大规模语料中检测和量化评论文本级别的话题漂移。研究者利用预训练语言模型为短文本生成上下文语义嵌入，分析了2006至2022年间127亿条Reddit评论，通过无监督方法识别随时间动态演化的主题聚类，并提出了空模型对比检验来过滤虚假动态。研究发现政治和社会争议性话题在嵌入空间中表现出显著的定向漂移，而音乐和体育等领域则相对稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态主题建模方法在大规模短文本语料上的语义漂移分析存在挑战，特别是如何在海量数据中有效检测话题随时间演化的方向性变化，需要一种能够从嵌入空间本身分析语义漂移和话语演化的方法论。

Method: 1) 利用预训练语言模型生成短文本的上下文语义嵌入；2) 对嵌入采用无监督方法识别动态演化的主题聚类；3) 对现有方法进行修改以支持大规模分析；4) 提出并实施空模型对比检验以过滤虚假的主题动态。

Result: 政治和社会争议性话题在嵌入空间中表现出显著的定向漂移，主题间距离随时间系统性变化且超出了空模型所能解释的范围；而音乐、体育等领域在嵌入空间中保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间本身可用于有效分析语义漂移和话语演化，且不同领域的话题漂移程度存在显著差异，政治社会类话题的演化比娱乐类话题更为活跃和方向性明显。

Abstract: We present a novel application of embedding-based dynamic topic modeling techniques to detect and quantify topic drift at the comment level in a massive corpus. By leveraging pretrained language models to generate contextualized semantic embeddings for short text, we analyzed 12.7 billion Reddit comments spanning 2006 to 2022. Using unsupervised methods on these embeddings, we identify dynamically evolving topic clusters over time. Our primary contribution is a methodology for analysis of semantic drift and discourse evolution in the embedding space itself. We also demonstrate modifications to existing methods that enable this analysis at scale, and we propose and demonstrate a null model comparison test to filter spurious dynamics. Key findings suggest that politically and socially contentious topics exhibit significant directional drift in embedding space, with inter-topic distances changing systematically over time beyond what the null model can explain, whereas domains such as music and sports remain comparatively stable.

</details>


### [154] [ChildSafeAds Shared Task 2026: Commercial Content in Child-Facing YouTube Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19165)
*Thales Bertaglia,Catalina Goanta,Gerasimos Spanakis,Gunes Acar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ChildSafeAds共享任务，该任务旨在分析YouTube视频中可能面向儿童和青少年的商业内容。数据集包含3,360个视频片段，每个片段配有转录、视频/频道信息及链接页面，系统需完成三个子任务：识别推广类型（ST1）、分配产品类别（ST2）和识别法律风险标志（ST3）。证据分为四个累积访问级别以便比较数据收集成本，45.5%的视频未正确使用平台广告披露方法，GPT-5.4和GPT-5.6-luna被用于生成标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着YouTube平台上面向儿童和青少年的商业内容日益增多，现有广告披露机制存在明显漏洞（45.5%的视频未正确使用"含付费推广"标签），且缺乏系统化的方法来自动识别和分类这类商业内容。作者希望通过构建标准化数据集和共享任务，推动对儿童可能接触的商业内容的检测、分类与风险评估研究。

Method: 基于SponsorBlock众包浏览器扩展收集3,360个视频片段（来自939个频道），将每个片段与其转录、视频/频道信息和描述中链接的销售或服务页面配对。任务分为三个子任务：ST1识别推广类型、ST2分配产品类别、ST3识别法律风险标志。证据划分为四个累积访问级别（从转录到链接页面），以支持成本-效益比较。使用GPT-5.4经专家组织团队迭代审查后生成标签，GPT-5.6-luna独立标注开发集。

Result: 建立了包含3,360个视频、939个频道的数据集，覆盖三个子任务和四个证据访问级别。关键发现是45.5%的视频未能正确使用平台内广告披露方法（"Includes paid promotion"标签），凸显了现有监管披露机制的不足。

Conclusion: 本文作为共享任务报告，描述了任务设计、数据构建和评估方法，为后续参与者提供基准框架。更新版本将加入参评系统和共享任务结果。该工作为儿童内容中的商业推广检测提供了标准化评估平台，揭示了广告披露合规性方面的显著问题。

Abstract: ChildSafeAds is a shared task on commercial content in YouTube videos likely to reach children and teenagers. It contains 3,360 videos from 939 channels. Each instance begins with a segment submitted to SponsorBlock, an open-source crowdsourced browser extension whose users mark sponsor segments so that others can skip them. We pair the segment with its available transcript, video and channel information, and a sales or service page linked from the video description. Systems determine what kind of offer is being promoted (ST1), assign product categories (ST2), and identify legal risk flags (ST3). The evidence is divided into four cumulative access levels, from the transcript to the linked page, so results can be compared against the cost of collecting the data. 45.5\% of videos in our data failed to properly use the in-platform ad disclosure method (the ``Includes paid promotion'' label). GPT-5.4 produced the labels after the expert organiser team reviewed samples and iterated on the taxonomy, prompts and model choices. GPT-5.6-luna independently labelled the development set. This report describes the task, data and evaluation. An updated version will add participating systems and shared-task results.

</details>


### [155] [SPADE: Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19197)
*Bo Liu,Simon Yu,Yiding Jiang,Ao Qu,Andrew Zhao,Zichen Liu,Junsu Kim,Zijian Zhou,Seungone Kim,Tongzheng Ren,Mickel Liu,Hanfei Yu,Zhaorun Chen,Weiyan Shi,Paul Pu Liang,Luke Zettlemoyer,Yejin Choi,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPADE提出了一个自博弈强化学习框架，其中单个LLM同时扮演环境设计器和推理智能体两种角色，通过遗憾信号驱动环境设计器生成自适应、多样化的训练环境，实现开放式自我改进。实验表明该方法在多个基准上超越固定环境基线，且优势随模型规模增大而增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体要实现持续自我改进，需要不断扩充多样化、自适应的目标池。然而现有的训练环境池（人工设计、静态合成或冻结验证器）在学习者规模扩展时无法保持目标分布的动态适应性，阻碍了开放式学习。

Method: SPADE框架包含两个角色：(1) 环境设计器：生成具有OpenAI Gym风格reset()/step()接口的完整长期训练环境（可执行代码），包含状态转换、奖励函数和验证代码；(2) 推理智能体：在生成的环境中学习行动。通过计算智能体在有/无私密提示下的奖励差距来估计遗憾，环境设计器据此优化以生成处于智能体能力边缘但可行的环境。关键组件包括：基于大规模预训练语料文档接地环境设计器，以及累积环境记忆。

Result: 扩展到30B参数模型时，SPADE在八个保留的数学、科学、代码和推理基准上平均比最强固定环境基线提升+5.3；在BFCL-v4多轮工具使用设置上提升+5.7，在ACEBench-Agent上提升+13.9；在游戏设置中，相对最强基线的优势随模型规模增长而扩大。

Conclusion: 通过将环境设计本身作为可学习组件，SPADE统一了推理问题和多步智能体工具使用场景，为开放式自我改进迈出了具体一步。环境设计的可学习性使训练环境池能随智能体能力动态演化，从而实现持续的自适应学习。

Abstract: Continuous self-improvement requires an ever-expanding pool of self-generated, diverse, adaptive goals. For language agents, existing training environment pools (hand-curated, statically synthesized, or frozen-verifier) keep the goal distribution fixed as the learner scales. We introduce SPADE (Self-Play in Adaptive Synthetic Executable Environments), a self-play RL framework in which a single LLM plays two roles: an Environment Designer that writes complete, long-horizon training environments as executable code with an OpenAI Gym-style reset()/step() interface, and a Reasoning Agent that learns to act in them. Each is a stateful, multi-turn environment (state transitions, reward functions, and verification code), so one interface spans reasoning problems and multi-step agentic tool use. The Reasoning Agent's regret is estimated using the gap between its reward with and without privileged hints; in optimizing this regret signal the Environment Designer learns to target environments at the edge of the agent's capabilities while keeping them feasible. Through extensive experimentation, we find several components critical to success: grounding the Environment Designer on documents sampled from a large pretraining corpus, and giving it an accumulated environment memory. Scaling to 30B-parameter models, SPADE improves over the strongest fixed-environment baseline by +5.3 on average across eight held-out math, science, code, and reasoning benchmarks, and lifts the tool-use setting by +5.7 on BFCL-v4 multi-turn and +13.9 on ACEBench-Agent; on the games setting, the margin over the strongest baseline grows with model scale. By making environment design itself a learnable component, SPADE takes a concrete step toward open-ended self-improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Demo: tfdrift - A Severity Taxonomy and Risk Classification Framework for Infrastructure Drift Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18173)
*Sudarshan Bhagvanthakur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对基础设施即代码（IaC）配置漂移的严重性分类体系，将漂移变化分为四个风险等级，并实现了开源工具tfdrift。在150多个AWS Terraform工作空间上的评估表明，该框架可将告警量减少73%，同时保留94%的安全相关变更。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的配置漂移检测方法将所有变化等同对待，导致告警疲劳，使运维人员可能忽略关键的安全修改。需要一种能够区分风险等级的分类机制来优先处理高风险的漂移。

Method: 提出了一个广义的漂移严重性分类体系，基于资源类型和属性级影响将变更分为四个风险等级；并将其实现为tfdrift开源框架，包含60多条可配置规则，覆盖AWS、Azure和GCP资源模式。

Result: 在150多个AWS Terraform工作空间上的评估显示，严重性过滤可将告警量减少73%，同时保留94%的安全相关变更，作为ML告警过滤的轻量级替代方案。

Conclusion: 基于规则驱动的严重性分类框架能够有效缓解告警疲劳，在减少告警噪音的同时保持对安全关键变更的高检出率，为IaC配置漂移检测提供了实用的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Infrastructure as Code (IaC) tools like Terraform have become the standard for declarative cloud resource management, yet configuration drift, where deployed infrastructure diverges from its declared state, remains a persistent operational and security challenge. Current detection approaches treat all changes equivalently, contributing to alert fatigue that causes operators to miss security-critical modifications. We propose a generalized severity taxonomy for infrastructure drift that classifies changes into four risk tiers based on resource type and attribute-level impact. We implement this taxonomy in tfdrift, an open-source classification framework with 60+ configurable rules covering AWS, Azure, and GCP resource patterns (evaluation reported here is AWS-focused). Evaluation across 150+ AWS Terraform workspaces demonstrates that severity filtering reduces alert volume by 73% while retaining 94% of security-relevant changes, offering a lightweight alternative to ML-based alert filtering. tfdrift is available at github.com/sudarshan8417/tfdrift.

</details>


### [157] [Reproducibility is Not Enough: Artifact Verifiability in Decentralized-Build Package Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18180)
*Oreofe Solarin,Kelechi Kalu,James C. Davis,Paschal Amusuo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了四个去中心化包管理生态系统中软件构件（artifacts）的可验证性问题，提出了独立验证者模型和分层等价比较模型，并通过构件验证流水线测量了这些生态系统的构件可验证性。研究发现，除构建确定性外，构件可验证性还受限于缺失的源代码和构建元数据、隐式发布转换以及非传统构建实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复现且可验证的构建能增强分布式软件构件的信任度，但构件验证不仅需要确定性构建，还需要恢复源代码状态、构建环境、依赖关系和构建指令。去中心化构建生态系统中，构件通过异构工具和维护者控制的工作流产生，元数据碎片化，导致构件能否被独立验证的问题尚不清楚。

Method: 作者定义了仅依赖注册表可衍生元数据的独立验证者模型，以及具有分层等价级别的构件比较模型，并将这些模型实现在构件验证流水线（Artifact Verification Pipeline）中，用于测量目标生态系统的构件可验证性。

Result: 研究发现，除了构建确定性之外，构件可验证性主要受限于缺失的源代码和构建元数据、隐式发布转换以及非传统构建实践。来源证明（provenance attestations）和嵌入式VCS元数据虽能改善验证，但未提供完整的重建规格。

Conclusion: 该研究识别出了具体的元数据缺口以及生态系统层面需要做出的改变，以使构件验证在包注册表规模上变得实用。

Abstract: Reproducible and verifiable builds increase trust in distributed software artifacts by enabling independent parties to detect artifacts produced by compromised build or release pipelines. However, artifact verification requires more than deterministic builds: a verifier must also recover the source state, build environment, dependencies, and build instructions that produced the artifact. Decentralized-build ecosystems make this difficult because artifacts are produced through heterogeneous tools, maintainer-controlled workflows, and fragmented metadata. As a result, it remains unclear how often artifacts in these ecosystems can be independently verified.
  This paper studies artifact verifiability across four popular decentralized-build package ecosystems. We define an independent verifier model that relies only on registry-derivable metadata and an artifact comparison model with tiered equivalence levels. We implement these models in an Artifact Verification Pipeline and use it to measure artifact verifiability across the target ecosystems. Our results show that, beyond build determinism, verifiability is limited by missing source and build metadata, implicit release transformations, and unconventional build practices. Provenance attestations and embedded VCS metadata improve verification, but they do not provide complete rebuild specifications. These findings identify concrete metadata gaps and ecosystem-level changes needed to make artifact verification practical at package-registry scale.

</details>


### [158] [What Makes Software Issue Resolution Tasks Difficult for Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18280)
*Ebtesam Al-Haque,Brittany Johnson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个测量框架，用于系统量化软件任务的结构属性与智能体成功率之间的关系。通过对最大的编码智能体轨迹开放数据集CoderForge-Preview进行大规模实证研究，发现任务难度可从静态特征高度预测（AUC=0.863），主要由补丁碎片化和仓库规模驱动，提示语言特征在中段难度任务中成为重要贡献因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管智能体系统在基准测试中快速饱和，但基准分数难以解读，因为我们缺乏对任务难度的控制和刻画。目前对什么使一个任务比另一个更难、以及任务难度在多大程度上可从静态任务属性预测，了解甚少。

Method: 在CoderForge-Preview（迄今最大的编码智能体轨迹开放数据集）上进行大规模实证研究，从任务补丁、仓库和提示中提取特征，使用集成方法、SHAP归因和效应量分析评估每个特征对任务结果的预测能力。

Result: 任务难度可从静态特征高度预测（AUC=0.863），主要由补丁碎片化和仓库规模驱动。提示语言特征在中段难度任务中进入重要贡献者行列，揭示了难度的分层结构。

Conclusion: 议题解决任务的难度编码在其结构中，这使静态的事前难度估计成为可能，并为构建难度受控的基准测试以评估智能体奠定基础。

Abstract: Background. Advances in agentic systems are simultaneously, and rapidly, saturating benchmarks. Despite this often discussed phenomena, benchmark scores remain difficult to interpret due to the lack of control and characterization of task difficulty. More specifically, we currently have little understanding of what makes one task harder than another, and to what extent task difficulty is predictable from static task properties. Aims. We propose a measurement framework to investigate and systematically quantify what structural properties of software tasks correspond to agent success rates for issue resolution tasks. Method. We conducted a large scale empirical study on CoderForge-Preview, the largest open dataset of coding agent trajectories to date, by extracting features across task patch, repository and prompt. We evaluated the predictive power of each feature against task outcomes using ensemble methods, SHAP attribution, and effect size analysis. Results We found that task difficulty is substantially predictable from static features (AU C = 0.863) and is largely driven by patch fragmentation and repository scale. Prompt linguistic features become visible among top contributors for tasks in the mid-band, revealing a layered structure of difficulty. Conclusion. The difficulty of an issue resolution task is encoded in its structure. This enables static, pre-hoc difficulty estimation and lays the groundwork for difficulty-controlled benchmark construction for evaluation of agents.

</details>


### [159] [Engine-Transfer-Bench: An Evidence-Based Benchmark for Document Compilation Engine Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18329)
*Prajwal S. Venkateshmurthy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 Engine-Transfer-Bench (ETB)，这是首个用于对比文档编译引擎（pdfLaTeX、XeLaTeX、LuaLaTeX、Tectonic、Typst、pandoc）的统一基准框架，包含1,784篇开放文档、四项任务（可靠性、延迟、文本一致性和失败分析），并提供跨平台（macOS、Ubuntu、Windows）的固定测试工具链和结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏统一的框架来帮助用户在多种文档编译引擎之间进行选择，导致选择过程依赖经验而非系统化的评估依据。

Method: 构建ETB基准：收集1,784篇开放文档，设计四项评估任务（可靠性、延迟、文本一致性、失败分析），使用固定测试工具链并在GitHub Actions上跨三种操作系统（macOS、Ubuntu、Windows）进行大规模编译测试（每主机4,211次编译）。同时提出S_pdf文本一致性度量指标，并构建ETB-Porta、推荐器和可移植性门控工具。

Result: Tectonic在跨平台编译成功率上最稳定（96.3%-97.2%，波动仅0.9个百分点）；经典TeX Live风格引擎因发行策略不同成功率波动达12-20个百分点。702个可移植LaTeX文档在所有引擎上100%成功，此时延迟成为主要选择因素；失败主要集中在107个引擎特定模板中，且失败本质上是架构性的（涉及字体、布局、资源），而非缺少宏包。S_pdf文本一致性度量在50对验证中达到94%的精确率。

Conclusion: ETB为文档编译引擎选择提供了系统化基准；Tectonic在跨平台可靠性上表现最优；对可移植文档而言延迟是决定性因素；研究发布ETB、ETB-Porta、推荐器及公开跨OS测试工具链作为共享基础设施。

Abstract: There is no shared framework for selecting among document compilation engines (pdfLaTeX, XeLaTeX, LuaLaTeX, Tectonic, Typst, and pandoc PDF backends). We present Engine-Transfer-Bench (ETB): 1,784 open documents, four tasks covering reliability, latency, text consistency, and failures, a pinned harness, and host-tagged multi-OS results. On GitHub Actions (N=4,211 compiles per host across macOS, Ubuntu, and Windows), Tectonic success is stable within 0.9 percentage points (96.3-97.2%), whereas classic TeX Live-style engines vary by 12-20 percentage points according to distribution policy (Ubuntu apt, MiKTeX auto-install, or macOS BasicTeX). On 702 portable LaTeX documents, the tested engines succeed at 100%, making latency the primary selection factor; failures concentrate in 107 engine-specific templates. Within ETB, failures are architectural, involving fonts, layout, and assets, rather than missing packages on a provisioned host. A 50-pair validation of the S_pdf text-consistency metric achieves 94% precision for real content divergence. We release ETB, ETB-Porta, a recommender and portability gate, and a public cross-OS harness as shared infrastructure.

</details>


### [160] [One Gate Is Not Enough: Composing Stateful Pre-Action Controls for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18360)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究Agentic AI系统中多重事前控制（权威门、资源门、证据门）之间的"修复诱导控制耦合"问题：一个控制施加的修复可能改变另一个控制所评估的动作、证据或上下文，从而使其先前的判断失效。作者形式化了这种耦合，提出"修复并重新门控"（remediate-and-regate）协议以恢复每次动作的可靠性，并证明两种修复算子不满足交换律。此外还发现受管辖证据缓冲区存在状态层面的类似问题，易受特定缺陷类别的投毒攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统在采取后果性动作时受多个控制门约束，但这些控制并非独立运作。一个控制施加的修复（如证据替换、资源预算降级）可能改变另一控制的评估输入，破坏系统整体可靠性。这一问题尚未被形式化研究，缺乏系统性的治理协议。

Method: 1) 形式化定义修复诱导控制耦合；2) 提出remediate-and-regate协议（在限定有界、幂等设置及其假设下保证每次动作的可靠性）；3) 使用有限模型检查器寻找两个修复算子（证据替换与资源预算降级）不交换的反例；4) 分析受管辖证据缓冲区在状态层面投毒脆弱性并提出两种缓解措施；5) 建立门控聚合的补偿条件、统一跨控制证据集，并诚实报告组合不产生新检测覆盖；6) 在确定性开放数据工件上组合三个已发布的引擎（未修改），对30个预注册种子进行实测。

Result: 两个修复算子被证明不交换（有限模型检查器找到具体反例）；证据缓冲区易受已声明未覆盖缺陷类别的投毒攻击，两种缓解只能减少而非消除暴露；在实证中，CH1-CH5在全部30个预注册种子上满足注册决策规则，CH6在W1工作流下满足但在较小的W2工作流下不满足（如实报告）。

Conclusion: 修复顺序属于控制平面的语义而非实现细节；当前设定下的可靠性与现实安全之间存在差距，作者明确声明这是机制演示而非生产流行性断言，显示了诚实报告局限性的科学态度。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems take consequential actions governed by more than one pre-action control at once: authority, resource, and evidence gates that can admit, degrade, or remediate an action before it executes. This paper's central object is remediation-induced control coupling: a remediation applied by one control can change the action, evidence, or context another control evaluates, invalidating that control's earlier judgment. We formalize this coupling and give a remediate-and-regate protocol that restores per-action soundness in the current bounded, idempotent setting under its stated assumptions. We further show that the two implemented remediation operators (evidence substitution and resource-budget downroute) do not commute -- a finite-model checker finds concrete counterexample instances -- making remediation order part of the control-plane semantics rather than an implementation detail. A governed evidence buffer that trusts its own most recent admitted write is a further instance of the same problem at the level of state -- current admissibility does not imply future reference trustworthiness -- and is vulnerable to poisoning from declared-uncovered defect classes; two mitigations reduce, not eliminate, that exposure. Supporting results establish the exact condition under which positive-weight linear aggregation of gate outcomes can compensate a member veto, a unified cross-control Evidence Set, and that composition manufactures no new detection coverage, reported honestly. Empirically, on a deterministic open-data artifact composing three published engines unmodified, CH1-CH5 meet their registered decision rules across all 30 pre-registered seeds; CH6 does so under W1 but not under the smaller W2 workflow, reported as such. This is a mechanism demonstration on open payload data with a synthetic metadata layer, not a claim about production prevalence.

</details>


### [161] [Building real-time digital twin instances with Function+Data Flow: user evaluation and extension for iterative pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18480)
*Eduardo de Conto,Blaise Genest,Arvind Easwaran,Nicholas Ng,Shweta Menon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出FDF（Function+Data Flow）视觉领域特定语言及其实施工具DesCartes Builder，用于AI驱动的数字孪生（DT）流水线的规范化建模。通过实证用户研究评估了其可用性与特性充分性，并基于研究结果提出分层扩展H-FDF以支持更复杂的迭代和模块化流水线（如双重训练）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生越来越多地利用AI/ML流水线来构建实时DT或用历史数据实例化，但工程化这些流水线仍然高度临时化：难以指定、验证和复用，缺乏专用工具支持。

Method: 1) 提出FDF视觉DSL，将函数（ML模型）显式表示，支持组合与复用；2) 实现DesCartes Builder集成建模环境，支持基于FDF的DT合成与验证；3) 开展实证用户研究，参与者实现代表性实时DT原型，通过定量与定性测量评估感知可用性及特性充分性；4) 基于研究发现提出H-FDF分层扩展。

Result: DesCartes Builder和FDF在广泛潜在用户群体中达到良好可用性水平，尤其适合目标受众（领域专家）。研究还揭示了两者具体的优势与待改进之处。

Conclusion: 集成式、模型驱动的平台是将基于AI的数字孪生工程转变为规范性建模实践的有前景方向。H-FDF扩展支持更复杂的DT流水线（如双重训练）的形式化规范。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) increasingly leverage artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) pipelines, both to build real-time DTs from high-fidelity simulations and to instantiate them with historical data. However, engineering these pipelines remains largely ad-hoc: pipelines are hard to specify, validate, and reuse, with scarce dedicated tooling. Function+Data Flow (FDF) addresses this by defining a visual domain-specific language (DSL) that represents functions (ML models) explicitly, enabling their composition and reuse. We implemented FDF in DesCartes Builder, an integrated modeling environment supporting FDF-based DT synthesis and validation.
  In this paper, we report on an empirical user study evaluating whether FDF and DesCartes Builder can make AI-based DT development more accessible and reliable. Participants implemented a representative real-time DT prototype within DesCartes Builder, and we measured perceived usability and feature adequacy through quantitative and qualitative measures. Our results indicate that DesCartes Builder and FDF achieve a good level of usability across a broad range of potential users, and particularly for the intended audience of domain experts. The study additionally surfaces concrete strengths and areas for improvement of both the tool and the underlying FDF framework. Informed by these findings, we propose H-FDF, a Hierarchical extension of FDF supporting iterative and modular pipelines, enabling the formal specification of more complex DT pipelines such as dual training. Our findings suggest that integrated, model-driven platforms are a promising direction to transform AI-based DT engineering into a disciplined modeling practice.

</details>


### [162] [SemaPLC: A Project-Grounded, Verification-Gated Agent Harness for PLC Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18565)
*Yanlun Tu,Huacan Wang,Ziyue Zhou,Jie Zhou,Ningyan Zhu,Ge Chen,Wangyi Chen,Tengfei Zhou,Yifan Zhou,Dasheng Yang,Xiaofeng Mou,Hui Zhang,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 SemaPLC，一个基于项目上下文且以验证为门控的智能体框架，用于在真实 PLC 项目中生成和集成可编程逻辑控制器（PLC）程序。与仅依赖模型自我判断不同，SemaPLC 仅在外部日志验证（涵盖规格、编译和运行时行为）全部通过后才宣布任务完成。在 117 个独立 POU 任务上平均严格通过率达 72.6%（七种模型中最高），在 65 个项目上下文任务上集成编译、静态行为和动态行为均取得最高均值。动态行为是最具区分度的指标：基线得分 22.4-31.4，而 SemaPLC 达到 52.2。结果表明执行（而非静态评分）才是检验生成控制逻辑是否真正有效的忠实标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型已能生成独立的 PLC 程序组织单元（POU），但生成逻辑是否能集成进现有 PLC 项目并正确运行，仅在有限测试中验证过。现有方法通常以模型自我判断为完成标准，缺乏严格的外部验证门槛。因此需要一个以项目为背景、以验证为门控的框架来衡量生成逻辑的真实可用性。

Method: SemaPLC 是一个由传统工具组装但受严格完成规则约束的智能体框架。它不会在模型自认为输出合格时停止，而是仅在外部日志检查确认三项标准全部通过后才声明任务完成：(1) 规格层检查——验证是否符合需求规格；(2) 编译层检查——确认生成代码能在真实项目中编译；(3) 运行时行为层检查——将生成逻辑和参考逻辑部署到实时 PLC 运行时，比较执行轨迹。框架包含独立 POU 任务和项目上下文任务两个评测轨道。

Result: 在 117 个独立 POU 任务上，SemaPLC 在全部七种评测模型上取得最高严格验证通过率（均值 72.6%）。在 65 个项目上下文任务上，在集成编译、静态行为和动态行为三个层面均取得最高均值。动态行为最具区分度：所有方法静态得分差距在 10 分以内，但动态得分差距显著，基线为 22.4-31.4，SemaPLC 达 52.2。验证门控机制在每个层面均提升了均值，尤其在最关键的运行时层面提升最显著。

Conclusion: 基于验证门控的框架能在每个层面提升 PLC 代码生成的性能，尤其在运行时行为层面提升最为显著。执行（真实运行时轨迹比较）而非静态评分，才是检验生成控制逻辑是否真正有效的忠实标准。SemaPLC 已开源。

Abstract: Programmable logic controllers (PLCs) run industrial plants, and large language models can already generate independent program organization units (POUs) for them. Whether such logic integrates into an existing PLC project and then runs correctly has been checked only in limited tests. We present \textsc{SemaPLC}, a project-grounded and verification-gated agent harness assembled from conventional tools but governed by a strict completion rule. Rather than stopping when the model judges its own output adequate, \textsc{SemaPLC} declares a task complete only when logged external checks confirm it. Those checks cover the specification, the compilation, and the behavior on a live runtime. On 117 independent-POU tasks matching existing benchmarks, it attains the highest strict verified pass rate on all seven models (72.6\% mean). On a project-context track of 65 tasks whose generated logic must compile and run inside a real project, it attains the highest mean on integrated compilation, static behavior, and dynamic behavior. Of the three layers, dynamic behavior is the most revealing. We measure it by deploying the generated and the reference logic to a live PLC runtime and comparing their executed traces. All methods fall within 10 static points of one another, whereas dynamic scores separate them sharply, from 22.4 to 31.4 for the baselines against 52.2 for \textsc{SemaPLC}. Overall, our verification-gated harness raises the mean at every layer and most sharply at runtime. Execution, not static scoring, is the faithful test of whether generated control logic actually works. \textsc{SemaPLC} is open-sourced at https://github.com/midea-ai/SemaPLC.

</details>


### [163] [AppEval: A Unified Benchmark for LLM-Based Mobile Application Repair in ArkTS, Swift, and Kotlin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18588)
*Bang Xie,Hao Liu,Zhenyu Shi,Yonghao Zhang,Senjian Zhang,Zhiyuan Peng,Xin Yin,Chenhao Ying,Yuan Luo,Wei Chen,Haiming Jin,Shaocong Long,Xu Liu,Zhe Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AppEval是一个针对移动应用仓库级LLM智能体修复的基准测试与评估框架，覆盖HarmonyOS/ArkTS、iOS/Swift和Android/Kotlin三个平台。其核心贡献是提出了"运行时感知"的验收标准：只有安装在设备上的应用在缺陷版本上触发断言失败、在修复后通过测试才算成功，基础设施故障被单独归类。在200个经审核的Android任务上，五个智能体的Pass@1介于22.00%到90.50%之间，差距达68.5个百分点，表明移动端修复性能强烈依赖于被评估的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的仓库级LLM智能体评估通常在构建主机上运行测试，忽略了移动应用特有的"构建-安装-启动-测试"边界问题。在这种边界下，缺失SDK、设备离线或断言前的崩溃都可能被误判为程序失败。因此需要一种能区分真实程序缺陷与基础设施故障的评估框架，以便公平、有意义地比较各智能体的修复能力。

Method: AppEval将隐藏行为测试与参考生产修复分离，验收条件是同一已安装应用目标在缺陷修订版本上必须达到断言失败、在修复后通过；基础设施故障作为独立结果类别。通过统一模式将这一契约映射到各平台的构建系统、运行时和测试运行器，并建立了包含200个已接受测试任务的Android审核数据集（来自24个可独立构建的仓库）。

Result: 在Android 200个任务上，五个智能体的Pass@1范围为22.00%到90.50%，最大差距达68.5个百分点。结论指出移动修复性能强烈依赖评估的智能体，并证明运行时感知的验收标准对有意义比较的必要性。但当前量化结果仅限Android，iOS和HarmonyOS的审核结果尚未完成。

Conclusion: AppEval证明了运行时感知的验收机制对于移动应用修复评估至关重要，避免了将基础设施故障误判为程序失败。不过，跨平台泛化结论需要在iOS和HarmonyOS上完成审核实验后才能得出，当前发现为Android特有。

Abstract: Repository-level LLM agents are typically evaluated on projects whose tests run on the build host. It remains unclear whether their repairs survive the mobile build-install-launch-test boundary, where a missing SDK, offline device, or pre-assertion crash can be mistaken for a program failure. We present AppEval, a benchmark and native-toolchain evaluation framework for mobile application repair across HarmonyOS/ArkTS, iOS/Swift, and Android/Kotlin. Each task separates a hidden behavior test from the reference production fix and is accepted only when the same installed-app target reaches an assertion failure on the defective revision and passes after the fix; infrastructure failures remain a distinct outcome. A common schema maps this contract to each platform's build system, runtime, and test runner. The audited Android partition contains 200 accepted instrumentation tasks from 24 independently buildable repositories. On these tasks, five agents achieve Pass@1 between 22.00% and 90.50%, a 68.50-percentage-point spread under the same dynamic oracle. These results show that mobile repair performance depends strongly on the evaluated agent while demonstrating why runtime-aware acceptance is necessary for meaningful comparison. The quantitative findings in this paper are Android-specific; audited iOS and HarmonyOS results are required before drawing cross-platform generalization conclusions.

</details>


### [164] [OdinEval: A Reproducible Benchmark for LLM-Based Program Repair in the Odin Programming Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18595)
*Bang Xie,Hao Liu,Zhiyuan Peng,Xin Yin,Senjian Zhang,Yuan Luo,Chenhao Ying,Haiming Jin,Wei Chen,Shaocong Long,Zhenyu Shi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OdinEval是一个面向Odin系统编程语言的可复现仓库级修复基准，包含168个过滤后的实例，每个实例绑定issue、基础/修复提交、gold patch、回归测试、历史工具链和执行记录。评测6个语言模型，Kimi-K3以66.7%的Resolved分领先，Qwen3.8-Max以96.4%的Repro分最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级修复基准主要集中在少数主流语言上，像Odin这样的系统编程语言几乎没有被测试覆盖，导致评测结果缺乏对系统语言的泛化能力验证。

Method: 从公开Odin仓库中的文档化缺陷构建可复现基准。每个实例将issue绑定到基础/修复提交、gold patch、特定回归测试、历史工具链及执行记录。准入标准要求测试在基础版本失败且在gold fix后通过；无可用开发者测试时，使用黑盒测试经三实例模型独立评审、双历史状态执行并根据版本化测试编写技能反馈修订。

Result: 在168个过滤后实例上统一协议评测6个语言模型。Kimi-K3取得最高Resolved分66.7%，Qwen3.8-Max取得最高Repro分96.4%。

Conclusion: OdinEval填补了系统语言在仓库级修复基准中的空白，发布了冻结数据、源码归档、容器、验证器、模型补丁和审计清单，为Odin语言的自动修复研究提供了可复现的基础设施。

Abstract: Repository-level repair benchmarks still center on a few mainstream languages, leaving systems languages such as Odin largely untested. We present OdinEval, a reproducible benchmark built from documented defects in public Odin repositories. Each instance binds an issue to base and fix commits, a gold patch, an issue-specific regression test, a historical toolchain, and execution records. Admission requires the test to fail on the base revision and pass after the gold fix. When no usable developer test exists, a black-box test is reviewed independently by three instances of the same model, executed in both historical states, and revised from recorded feedback under a versioned Test Writing Skill. We evaluate six language models on 168 filtered instances under one shared protocol. Kimi-K3 records the highest Resolved score at 66.7%, while Qwen3.8-Max has the highest Repro score at 96.4%. The release includes frozen data, source archives, containers, validators, model patches, and audit manifests.

</details>


### [165] [Code Health in LLM-Based Test Generation: Effectiveness and Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18645)
*Freya Wirdemann,Markus Borg,Nadim Hagatulah,Adam Tornhill*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了由LLM驱动的编码代理生成的单元测试效果与代码可维护性（通过CodeScene的CodeHealth衡量）之间的关系。研究发现CodeHealth对LLM生成测试的有效性提供了微弱但一致的信号，且与输入token数量负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明AI工具在高质量、易于维护的源代码上表现更好，但尚未系统研究代码可维护性如何影响LLM生成的单元测试的有效性。

Method: 使用CodeScene的CodeHealth（CH）衡量代码可维护性水平，通过传统覆盖率指标和变异得分评估LLM生成的单元测试有效性，覆盖Python、Java和C++三种语言，并使用常见的工业tokenizer研究不同CH水平代码到输入token的转换关系。

Result: CH提供了微弱但一致的LLM生成测试有效性信号，且与输入token数量呈负相关关系。

Conclusion: 研究结果进一步证明代码可维护性与基于LLM的软件开发之间存在关联，提示代码质量可能影响LLM代理在测试生成任务上的表现。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by Large Language Models (LLMs) are now prominent in software engineering. Previous work has shown that AI tools perform better on high-quality source code that is easy to maintain. In this study, we investigate how the effectiveness of LLM-generated unit tests varies across maintainability levels measured by CodeScene's CodeHealth (CH). We assess test effectiveness using traditional coverage metrics and mutation score across Python, Java, and C++. Moreover, we study how code with different levels of CH translates into input tokens using common industrial tokenizers. Our results suggest that CH provides a weak but consistent signal of LLM-generated test effectiveness and is negatively correlated with input-token count. These findings provide further evidence for a relationship between maintainability and LLM-based software development.

</details>


### [166] [Flama: a Python framework for development and deployment of production-ready APIs, machine learning, and LLM services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18733)
*José A. Perdiguero López,Miguel A. Durán-Olivencia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Flama是一个基于ASGI的开源Python框架，采用类型驱动、异步优先的编程模型，统一了REST API开发、预测模型服务和生成式AI推理。框架包含七个子系统：依赖注入、可插拔模式层、自动CRUD生成器、便携二进制模型格式(.flm)、多后端LLM服务器、Rust加速核心和MCP模块，并内置JWT认证、分页、WebSocket、SSE/NDJSON流式传输等能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架通常将Web API开发、机器学习模型服务和LLM推理分离在不同的工具链中，开发者需要在多个框架和平台之间切换，缺乏一个统一架构来支持从REST API到生成式AI的完整开发与部署需求。

Method: 提出Flama框架，基于ASGI规范，通过七大子系统实现统一架构：1) 基于类型注解的组件依赖注入；2) 支持Pydantic/Marshmallow/Typesystem的统一模式层；3) SQLAlchemy表自动生成REST端点的CRUD生成器；4) .flm便携二进制格式打包多种ML框架模型实现零代码部署；5) 支持vLLM和MLX的多后端LLM服务器，暴露四种wire协议；6) Rust加速核心（经Maturin编译）处理路由、JSON编码等；7) MCP模块将应用转变为JSON-RPC 2.0的MCP服务器。

Result: 框架实现了REST API开发、模型服务和LLM推理的统一，内置JWT认证、两种分页策略、后台任务、WebSocket、SSE/NDJSON流式传输、OpenAPI 3.2.0文档生成以及CLI工具。通过与现有框架、模型服务平台和LLM推理引擎的对比分析展示了其差异化定位。

Conclusion: Flama提供了一个融合Web开发、机器学习服务和LLM应用的开源统一框架，通过模块化子系统设计和多后端支持，旨在降低生产级AI应用开发的复杂性和部署成本。

Abstract: We present Flama, an open-source Python framework for developing and deploying production-ready web APIs, machine learning services, and large-language-model (LLM) applications. Built on the Asynchronous Server Gateway Interface (ASGI), Flama offers a type-driven, async-first programming model that unifies REST API development, predictive model serving, and generative AI inference in one architecture.
  It is organised around seven subsystems: a component-based dependency injection system resolving handler parameters from type annotations at startup; a pluggable schema layer supporting Pydantic, Marshmallow and Typesystem behind a single adapter; an automatic CRUD generator turning a SQLAlchemy table and a schema class into REST endpoints backed by the Repository and Unit of Work patterns; a portable binary format (.flm) packaging models from scikit-learn, TensorFlow, PyTorch and Hugging Face Transformers with their metadata for zero-code deployment; a multi-backend LLM server running vLLM (Linux/CUDA) or MLX (Apple Silicon) and exposing four wire protocols (OpenAI, Anthropic, Ollama, and a native streaming dialect) through a shared codec; a Rust-accelerated core compiled via Maturin for routing, JSON encoding, compression and parsing; and a Model Context Protocol module turning any application into an MCP server over JSON-RPC 2.0.
  Built-in capabilities include JWT authentication, two pagination strategies, background tasks in threads or processes, WebSocket endpoints, Server-Sent Event and NDJSON streaming, OpenAPI 3.2.0 generation from handler signatures, and a command-line interface for running applications and for serving, packaging and inspecting models.
  We describe the architecture, present the programming model through worked examples, and compare Flama with existing frameworks, model serving platforms and LLM inference engines.

</details>


### [167] [Contract-Aware Rescue of a Drifted Isabelle Development: The Double-Tank Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18822)
*Jim Woodcock,Gabriel Leite,Augusto Sampaio,Ran Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在交互式定理证明器中的证明提出，发现"构建成功"并不保证验证任务被保留。通过对一个采样数据双罐控制器的Isabelle开发进行回顾性审计，发现100个原始声明中有16处实质性修改，包括被削弱的端到端保证定理。作者使用CAPRI（契约感知的证明修复工具）结合Isabelle接受性和独立契约检查来管理重构，成功在原始九理论结构中完成了所有十个范围内的义务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可以提出交互式定理证明器的证明，但成功的构建（build）无法证明周围的验证任务被保留。现有评估方法忽略了证明过程中可能发生的规范弱化或声明修改，需要更严格的验证方法。

Method: 在Isabelle开发的采样数据双罐控制器上，进行回顾性审计检查原始声明与最终声明的差异，并使用CAPRI工具（契约感知的证明修复工具）结合Isabelle的接受性检查和机器可读编辑契约的独立检查来治理重构过程。

Result: 初步工作从9个理论10个未完成义务开始，增长到16理论的构建（无sorry、oops、公理化或oracle使用），积累了23个稳定和36个破坏的证明状态。审计发现16处声明修改，包括一个弱化的端到端保证定理。通过CAPRI重构，在原始九理论结构中完成了所有10个范围义务。次要重放由合著者复制了R10构建、契约检查、控制测试和主要审计发现。端到端验证仍不完整。

Conclusion: LLM提出的证明需要严格的事后审计来确保验证任务被保留，契约感知的证明修复工具（如CAPRI）是有效的治理手段。独立的契约检查比单纯依赖定理证明器的接受性更可靠。操作端到端验证仍然未完成，需要扩展契约来连接操作执行与定量轨迹契约。

Abstract: Large language models can propose proofs for interactive theorem provers, but a successful build does not show the surrounding verification task was preserved. We study this problem in an Isabelle development of a sampled-data double-tank controller. The work began with nine theories and ten unfinished obligations, grew to a 16-theory build without sorry, oops, added axiomatisation, or oracle use, and accumulated 23 stable and 36 broken proof states. A retrospective audit found material changes in 16 of the 100 original declarations, including a weakened end-to-end assurance theorem that assumed three of the four requirements in its conclusion. We used CAPRI, a contract-aware proof-repair tool, to govern a reconstruction by combining Isabelle acceptance with an independent check of repository changes against machine-readable edit contracts. The reconstruction discharged all ten scoped obligations within the original nine-theory structure. A secondary replay by a co-author reproduced the R10 build, contract checks, control tests, and principal audit findings; independent replication remains future work. Operational end-to-end verification remains incomplete: we still need to connect operational executions to the reconstructed quantitative trace contract, a task requiring an extended contract.

</details>


### [168] [SkillForge: Self-Distilling Agents for Project-Specific Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18933)
*Silin Chen,Han Li,Xiaodong Gu,Yuling Shi,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SkillForge是一个自蒸馏框架，通过重新实现仓库中测试覆盖的核心功能来合成项目特定问题，并将解决这些合成问题过程中获得的可复用知识提炼为实体相关的技能，以提升大语言模型在特定仓库中的软件问题解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的智能体在自动化软件问题解决中表现优异，但缺乏项目特定知识，难以解决特定仓库中的问题。现有自进化方法依赖历史问题解决信号或产生高昂的测试时探索成本，因此需要一种更主动、更高效地获取项目特定知识的方法。

Method: SkillForge采用自蒸馏框架：首先通过重新实现仓库中测试覆盖的核心功能来合成项目特定问题，然后让模型解决这些合成问题，在此过程中将可复用的项目特定知识蒸馏为实体锚定的技能，并将其与相关仓库实体关联，供未来解决真实问题时使用。

Result: 在开源和闭源模型上的大量实验表明，SkillForge在问题解决性能上一致优于强基线方法，证明了主动获取项目特定知识能显著提升下游软件问题解决能力。

Conclusion: 在解决真实问题之前主动获取项目特定知识，能够有效提升软件问题解决的性能，SkillForge的自蒸馏方法为这一方向提供了有效且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based agents have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in automated software issue resolution, yet they often struggle to resolve issues in a specific repository because they lack project-specific knowledge. Existing self-evolving approaches acquire such knowledge from repository history or online repair trajectories, but they either depend on available historical issue-resolution signals or incur substantial per-issue test-time exploration cost. In this paper, we propose SkillForge, a self-distillation framework that proactively acquires project-specific knowledge from the repository itself. Instead of waiting for real issues to expose project-specific knowledge gaps, SkillForge synthesizes project-specific issues by re-implementing test-covered core functionalities of the repository. By resolving these synthetic issues, SkillForge distills reusable project-specific knowledge into entity-grounded skills and associates them with relevant repository entities for future issue resolution. Extensive experiments using both open-source and closed-source models show that SkillForge consistently improves issue resolution performance over strong baselines. These results demonstrate that proactively acquiring project-specific knowledge before solving real issues substantially improves downstream software issue resolution.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Position: Collusion Risks Among AI Reasoning Agents Justify Certification Requirements for Making Market Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18078)
*Matthew Riemer,Tommaso Tosato,Amin Memarian,Maximilian Puelma Touzel,Glen Berseth,Irina Rish,Guillaume Dumas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张具有链式思维推理能力的AI智能体天然倾向于合谋行为，应在影响经济市场决策前获得行为认证。作者通过DeepSeek-R1在伯特兰寡头定价领域的实验，证明智能体即使在人类提示不要合谋的情况下仍表现出默示合谋倾向，且其推理过程可被操纵而不被语义检测到。因此，基于代表性情境中观察到的行为进行认证是防止合谋的必要手段，同时初步证据表明可将智能体导向高效竞争均衡，但全面行为认证的开发仍是部署前的前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
